### [1] [LaMET-Agent: An Agent Framework for Large-Momentum Effective Theory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32225)
*Jinchen He,Xiangyu Jiang,Fei Yao,Dian-Jun Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出lamet-agent——一个开源的大型语言模型（LLM）智能体框架，用于自动化、可复现、可审查地执行基于大动量有效理论（LaMET）的格点QCD部分子物理分析流程，已验证其在π介子PDFs、DA及K介子DA等四类端到端计算中与已有结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LaMET框架下部分子物理计算流程复杂、依赖专家经验、重复劳动多、可复现性差的问题。

Method: 构建基于LLM的智能体系统，将LaMET标准分析流程（关联子计算、重整化、傅里叶变换、微扰匹配、连续极限/物理π质量/无穷动量外推、结果审查）编码为可执行、模块化、可审计的流水线。

Result: 成功实现并验证了对胶子无关与库仑规范下π介子部分子分布函数（PDFs）、π/K介子分布振幅（DAs）共四组端到端计算，结果与已发表工作一致；支持自动结果审查与全流程日志追踪。

Conclusion: lamet-agent显著提升了LaMET部分子计算的自动化程度、可复现性与可审查性，为未来扩展至TMDs、GTMDs和胶子分布函数奠定了工程基础。

Abstract: Large-momentum effective theory (LaMET) provides a first-principles framework for computing the $x$ dependence of light-cone parton distributions from lattice QCD. Over the past decade, theoretical and numerical advances have established a mature multi-stage workflow for systematic calculation of parton physics, although its implementation still requires expert judgment and substantial repeated effort. We present lamet-agent, an open-source large language model (LLM) agent framework that organizes this workflow into an executable, reproducible, and inspectable analysis pipeline. The present release supports collinear quark distributions and implements correlator analysis, renormalization, Fourier transformation, perturbative matching, continuum, physical pion mass and infinite-momentum extrapolations, and automated result review. We validate it on four end-to-end analyses: pion parton distribution functions in the gauge-invariant and Coulomb-gauge formulations, and pion and kaon distribution amplitudes, obtaining results consistent with the published calculations. Extensions to transverse-momentum-dependent distributions, generalized transverse-momentum-dependent distributions, and gluonic distribution functions are planned for subsequent releases.

</details>


### [2] [Measuring Mass Splittings in Baryon SU(2) Multiplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34647)
*Constantia Alexandrou,Simone Bacchio,Antonio Evangelista,Christos Iona,Christian Schneider*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文使用RM123方法，在接近物理夸克质量的twisted-mass格点QCD组态上，首次在领头阶计算了自旋1/2和3/2重子SU(2)二重态内的同位旋破缺质量劈裂，仅考虑价夸克贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着格点QCD可观测量精度进入百分比量级，电磁相互作用与u-d夸克质量差导致的同位旋破缺修正已不可忽略，亟需系统纳入。

Method: 采用RM123方法，在扭质量费米子格点组态上，于同位旋破缺参数的领头阶（linear order）展开下，仅计算价夸克圈图贡献的质量劈裂。

Result: 给出了质子-中子、Σ⁺-Σ⁰、Σ⁰-Σ⁻等自旋1/2重子二重态，以及Δ⁺⁺-Δ⁺、Δ⁺-Δ⁰、Δ⁰-Δ⁻等自旋3/2重子四重态的质量劈裂的初步格点结果。

Conclusion: RM123方法可有效分离并量化同位旋破缺效应；当前领头阶价夸克近似结果为后续包含海夸克与高阶修正提供了基准。

Abstract: The inclusion of isospin-breaking corrections arising from electromagnetic interactions and the up-down quark mass difference is becoming increasingly important in lattice QCD calculations, as the precision of observables is at or approaching the percent level. In this context, the RM123 method has been employed in recent years to take into account such isospin corrections. In this work, we study baryon mass splittings using the RM123 approach. Preliminary results are presented for the mass splittings within spin-1/2 and spin-3/2 baryon SU(2) multiplets, obtained using twisted-mass ensembles tuned close to physical quark masses. We consider only valence contributions, with the splittings computed at leading order in the isospin-breaking parameters.

</details>


### [3] [The Peskin-Takeuchi $S$ parameter and vector meson decay constants in $N_f=8$ QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35371)
*Yasumichi Aoki,Tatsumi Aoyama,Ed Bennett,Toshihide Maskawaa,Kohtaroh Miura,Hiroshi Ohki,Enrico Rinaldi,Akihiro Shibata,Koichi Yamawaki,Takeshi Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用格点规范理论研究了八味QCD（SU(3)带8种轻味费米子）中的Peskin-Takeuchi S参数，作为行走型技术色动力学候选模型；提出基于流关联函数一阶时间矩的新S计算方法，与传统真空极化法一致；系统估计有限体积修正后，得到S ≈ 0.281(5)(17)，略低于缩放后的2+1味QCD值；L₁₀ʳ(M_ρ)、F_ρ/F_π比值及谱函数求和规则均支持其类QCD行为，并给出技术ρ介子衰变宽度上限Γ_ρ/M_ρ ≲ 0.24/N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验八味SU(3)规范理论作为行走型技术色动力学（walking technicolor）候选模型的电弱精确可观测量兼容性，特别是Peskin-Takeuchi S参数是否足够小以满足电弱精度实验约束。

Method: 采用格点规范理论模拟；引入基于矢量-轴矢流关联函数第一时间矩的S参数新计算方法；结合传统真空极化方法；通过多体积格点数据并借助手征微扰论启发的公式估算有限体积修正；同时提取矢量/轴矢介子衰变常数F_ρ、F_{a₁}及Gasser-Leutwyler低能常数L₁₀ʳ(M_ρ)。

Result: 在最小费米子质量下，单二重态贡献的S = 0.281(5)(17)，略小于缩放2+1味QCD的~0.35；有限体积修正后S未随费米子质量减小而单调压低；L₁₀ʳ(M_ρ)与真实QCD相近；F_ρ/F_π稳定且符合KSRF关系；ρ→WW衰变宽度满足Γ_ρ/M_ρ ≲ 0.24/N；Weinberg谱函数与Das-Mathur-Okubo求和规则自洽成立。

Conclusion: 八味QCD展现出类QCD的低能动力学特征，S参数处于可接受范围但未显著压低，表明其作为行走型技术色动力学模型需谨慎评估BSM构造；结果为电弱对称性破缺机制的格点检验提供了关键非微扰基准。

Abstract: We use lattice gauge theory to investigate the Peskin-Takeuchi S parameter in SU(3) gauge theory with eight light fundamental fermions (eight-flavor QCD), a candidate for walking technicolor. We develop a new approach to computing S using the first time moment of current correlators, in addition to the conventional vacuum-polarization method, and obtain consistent results. Finite-volume (FV) corrections are estimated using data at multiple lattice volumes and a formula motivated by chiral perturbation theory. At the smallest fermion mass, the one-doublet contribution is S = 0.281(5)(17), smaller than the value $\sim 0.35$ in scaled-up 2+1-flavor QCD (without subtracting the SM contribution). However, contrary to naive expectations, S toward smaller fermion masses is not necessarily suppressed once FV corrections are included. The resulting Gasser-Leutwyler parameter $L_{10}^r(M_ρ)$ is similar to that in real-world QCD. We also determine the vector and axial-vector meson decay constants, $F_ρ$ and $F_{a_1}$. The ratio $F_ρ/F_π$ is stable against the fermion mass and consistent with the KSRF estimate, implying a $ρππ$ coupling similar to that in real-world QCD. We predict the techni-rho decay width into weak bosons to be $Γ_ρ/M_ρ\lesssim 0.24/N$, where N is the number of weak doublets. We further find that the rho- and $a_1$-dominated Weinberg spectral-function and Das-Mathur-Okubo sum rules are satisfied, yielding S and $L_{10}^r(M_ρ)$ consistent with our main results. We discuss implications for BSM model building.

</details>


### [4] [Multilevel Plaquette-Space Sampling for Lattice Gauge Theories with Local Constraint Solves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35722)
*Ankur Singha,Jacob Finkenrath,Karl Jansen,Vipul Arora,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层级归一化流（normalizing flow）方法，在满足Bianchi约束的前提下直接在plaquette空间进行生成式采样，显著优于传统link-space生成模型，尤其在弱耦合极限下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒙特卡洛方法在格点规范理论中存在临界慢化和拓扑冻结等问题；而现有生成模型多工作于link空间，其长程关联在弱耦合下加剧，不利于建模；plaquette空间虽具局域作用量优势，但受精确Bianchi约束限制，难以直接建模。

Method: 构建一种多层级归一化流架构，通过粗到细的因子分解将全局耦合的Bianchi约束转化为一系列局部求解问题：每一层级中新生成的变量至多影响四个已确定的plaquette，且约束问题规模与格点尺寸无关。

Result: 在2D/4D U(1)与2D SU(2)格点规范理论中验证了该Plaquette-Space Sampler（PSS）的有效性；相比link-space基线模型，采样效率显著提升，且在弱耦合区域优势随耦合常数减小而增强。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了对Bianchi约束的精确、可扩展、高效生成式建模，为连续极限下的第一性原理计算（尤其在渐近自由理论中）提供了新工具。

Abstract: Lattice gauge theories are an important class of physical models with high-dimensional structured distributions that underpin first-principles calculations in particle, nuclear, and condensed-matter physics. Recent advances in generative modeling have opened new avenues for sampling Boltzmann distributions in lattice gauge theories, offering the potential to alleviate limitations of traditional Monte Carlo methods, including critical slowing down and topological freezing. However, generative samplers for lattice gauge theories are typically constructed in link space, where correlations become increasingly long-ranged toward weak coupling, posing a challenge for learning. Plaquettes provide a more natural representation, as the action is local in these variables. However, exact Bianchi constraints restrict them to a lower-dimensional manifold, complicating direct generative modeling. We introduce a multilevel normalizing-flow construction that samples directly in plaquette space while satisfying these constraints exactly. The key idea is a coarse-to fine factorization that transforms a globally coupled constraint problem into a sequence of local solves: at each refinement, every determined plaquette depends on at most four newly generated variables, while the size of the constraint problem remains independent of the lattice size. We validate the construction for $U(1)$ in two and four dimensions and for $SU(2)$ in two dimensions. Our multilevel Plaquette-Space Sampler (PSS) substantially outperforms link-space baselines, with the advantage increasing toward weak coupling, where the gauge coupling becomes small. This regime is particularly challenging for generative sampling and, in asymptotically free gauge theories, is relevant to continuum studies.

</details>

### [5] [Cardy random matrix theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31825)
*Marco Ambrosini,Alexandre Belin,Jan de Boer,Julian Sonner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个'Cardy随机矩阵模型'，其唯一输入是S-模不变性约束；通过在固定宽度能量窗口上粗粒化来正则化该约束，得到一个单随机矩阵的势能，其本征值对应二维共形场论中原初算符的标度维数；该势能驱动粗粒化态密度在黑洞阈值之上呈现Cardy分布；蒙特卡洛模拟验证了这一行为，并同时揭示了邻近本征值满足GUE统计；模型展现出宏观、介观与微观三重尺度层次：宏观尺度由窗口大小决定，态占据冻结且谱被刚性固定为Cardy分布；介观尺度（窗口内）Vandermonde排斥使本征值按随机矩阵理论重新排布；微观尺度（平均能级间距）才分辨单个能级；模型定义了一个有效Thouless时间，等于粗粒化窗口能量宽度的倒数；该模型为在计算机上求解三维量子引力的矩阵/张量模型对偶迈出第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从最大无知原则出发，仅以S-模不变性作为基本约束，构建一个能再现二维CFT热谱（Cardy公式）与随机性（能级统计）双重特征的随机矩阵模型，进而为三维量子引力的矩阵/张量模型对偶提供可计算框架。

Method: 基于S-模不变性构造粗粒化约束，导出单随机矩阵的有效势；采用Monte Carlo数值模拟研究本征值统计；系统分析宏观（窗口尺度）、介观（窗口内）与微观（能级间距）三重尺度下的谱响应；提取有效Thouless时间并确立其与粗粒化窗口宽度的倒数关系。

Result: 粗粒化态密度精确复现Cardy分布（含黑洞阈值间隙）；邻近能级间距分布符合Gaussian Unitary Ensemble（GUE）；存在清晰的三重尺度分离：宏观尺度下谱被刚性固定，介观尺度下Vandermonde排斥主导，微观尺度下能级离散性显现；定义并验证了由粗粒化窗口能量宽度决定的有效Thouless时间。

Conclusion: Cardy随机矩阵模型成功统一了CFT热谱的确定性（Cardy公式）与量子混沌的随机性（GUE统计），其多尺度结构自然导出有效Thouless时间，为三维量子引力的矩阵模型数值实现提供了首个原理性可行方案。

Abstract: We construct and study a ``Cardy random matrix model'': a random matrix theory whose only input, in a maximal-ignorance prescription, is the constraint of $\mathbb{S}$-modular invariance. We regularize this constraint by coarse-graining over energy windows of fixed width, and obtain a potential for a single random matrix whose eigenvalues play the role of the scaling dimensions of primaries in a 2d CFT. The potential drives the coarse-grained density of states to the Cardy profile above a gap at the black hole threshold. We perform Monte Carlo simulations to confirm this behavior and find good agreement for the coarse-grained density of states, while at the same time revealing GUE statistics for nearby eigenvalues. We identify the scaling limit in which these phenomena emerge. In this limit, the model realizes a hierarchy of scales: at the macroscopic scale given by the window size, the occupations are frozen and the bootstrap constraint rigidly fixes the spectrum to the Cardy profile, while at the mesoscopic scale (inside a window) the Vandermonde repulsion rearranges the eigenvalues as in a random matrix theory. It is only at the microscopic scale of the mean level spacing that the individual levels are resolved. Accordingly, our random matrix model displays an effective Thouless time, set by the inverse energy width of the coarse-graining window. Our model provides a first step towards solving the matrix/tensor model dual to 3D quantum gravity on a computer.

</details>

*Pietro Benetti Genolini,Florian Gaar,Jerome P. Gauntlett,Jaeha Park,James Sparks*

Main category: hep-th

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个普适、解析且可计算的超对称定位框架，以统一处理M理论在AdS4×X7背景下的微扰路径积分，尤其针对ABJM及更广义的3d N=2超共形场论（如V^{5,2}）在任意几何与化学势下的精确配分函数问题。

Method: 采用十一维超引力中的相对等变Chern–Simons耦合构造定位作用量，并利用M2-膜背景下的切-余切特征类计算一圈行列式；结合等变上同调与超对称定位技术，将路径积分严格约化为有限维等变通量空间上的积分。

Result: 1) ABJM模型在任意squashing参数与化学势下的全微扰配分函数闭式解；2) ABJM拓扑扭曲指数的新解析表达式（对应AdS4黑洞熵）；3) V^{5,2}理论中Airy常数的首次解析推导；4) Type IIA极限下全亏格(topological string constant map)的等变推广；5) 若干积分关联函数的显式结果。

Conclusion: 该定位公式建立了M理论微扰计算与三维超共形场论精确可观测量之间的严格桥梁，不仅复现所有已知场论结果，还提供了此前不可达参数区域的全新解析预言，标志着三维AdS/CFT中微扰量子引力计算进入封闭解析时代。

Abstract: We propose a supersymmetric localization formula for the local perturbative contribution to the M-theory path integral, reducing it to an integral over equivariant flux data. Relative equivariant extensions of the eleven-dimensional Chern--Simons couplings determine the localized action, while for M2-brane backgrounds a tangent-cotangent character computes the one-loop determinant. This gives the first closed form expression for the complete perturbative ABJM partition function at general squashing and chemical potentials, and new analytic results for the ABJM topologically twisted index describing black holes. Applied to the non-toric $V^{5,2}$ theory, the same prescription yields a closed analytic Airy constant previously known only numerically. Our formulas reproduce all available independent field theory results, and provide analytic predictions at general parameters where no field theory answer is currently known. We derive an all-genus result for the Type IIA expansion which is an equivariant form of the usual topological string constant map result, and also extract integrated correlators.

</details>


### [7] [Nonlinear electroweak sphaleron clouds around Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31845)
*Carlos Herdeiro,Jutta Kunz,Hendrik Mennenga,Eugen Radu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr黑洞与电弱sphaleron之间的相互作用，构造了在Schwarzschild和Kerr黑洞背景下的非线性sphaleron云（probe limit），分析其存在域、质量间隙及分支结构，并给出Schwarzschild背景下精确Yang-Mills构型上的首阶Higgs解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义相对论（Kerr黑洞）与标准模型（电弱sphaleron）这两大现代物理支柱解之间的非微扰耦合，检验强引力场中电弱拓扑结构的稳定性与新现象。

Method: 采用probe limit近似，在固定黑洞背景（Schwarzschild/Kerr度规）上求解SU(2)×U(1)电弱规范-Higgs系统的非线性场方程；结合数值打靶法与解析渐近分析，刻画sphaleron云的分支结构与质量谱；对Schwarzschild情形推导Higgs场在精确YM解上的闭式首阶解。

Result: 发现sphaleron云具有有限质量间隙，不退化为线性云（区别于超辐射云）；存在多支非平凡解结构；获得Schwarzschild背景下Higgs场的闭式首阶解，依赖于精确的纯Yang-Mills构型。

Conclusion: 电弱sphaleron可在黑洞周围形成稳定的非线性拓扑云，其性质由规范-希格斯耦合与时空曲率共同决定，不属超辐射机制范畴；该结果为引力-电弱统一的非微扰现象提供了新范例。

Abstract: Kerr black holes and electroweak sphalerons are remarkable solutions of two pillars of modern physics: General Relativity and the Standard Model of particle physics, respectively. In this work, we investigate their interplay by constructing nonlinear sphaleron clouds around Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes in the probe limit. We first revisit static and spinning (multi-)sphalerons in flat spacetime, using the measured electroweak parameters. We then explore the domain of existence and physical properties of the corresponding black-hole configurations, revealing a rich branch structure. Remarkably, the sphaleron clouds retain a finite mass gap and do not approach linear clouds within the families studied, distinguishing them from the familiar clouds associated with the onset of superradiant instabilities. We also obtain a closed-form leading-order Higgs solution on an exact Yang-Mills configuration in a Schwarzschild background.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum vacuum interaction between semitransparent plates in a Pöschl-Teller background: an exact relative TGTG formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31866)
*Jose M. Muñoz-Castañeda,Inés Cavero-Peláez,Gonzalo Sancho-Garrido*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了在单孤子Pöschl-Teller背景场中，两个半透明Dirac-delta平板间一阶量子真空相互作用的精确背景修饰TGTG公式；该相互作用通过扣除相同背景下的孤立单板贡献的相对行列式定义，能量依赖于各板位置及其间距；对排斥性平板，可归一化的零模不导致相对相互作用能的红外发散；并进一步推广至平面板情形，分析了位置依赖的相互作用力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建背景场中半透明δ势平板间量子真空相互作用的严格、可解模型，以澄清零模贡献与红外行为的关系，并推广至更一般几何构型。

### [40] [Microscopic Organization of BPS Black-Hole States in the D1--D5--P CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35688)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 结合组合表示论（循环分拆）、大N渐近分析、精确谱计算与有限绕数标量算符的两点关联函数解析，对比extremal-BTZ几何预言与CFT动力学响应。

Result: 1) fortuity态不被循环分拆唯一分类；2) 固定电荷大N系综主导贡献来自单长循环（N−O(1)）；3) 未凝聚区存在精确非引力子熵子空间；4) 超临界区形成短弦玻色凝聚；5) 长循环关联函数在固定时间趋近extremal-BTZ响应，时间尺度t=O(√N)分辨有限绕数，t=O(N)重现圆周对称性，相同循环几何态仍由谱权重可区分。

Conclusion: D1-D5 CFT中BPS态的fortuity与循环结构共同编码全息几何，但二者非一一对应；长弦主导统计行为体现强耦合引力图像，而探针动力学的时间分层结构揭示CFT中隐含的类时空几何尺度。

Abstract: We study BPS states in the D1--D5 CFT through their fortuity, cycle structure, and probe response. Explicit fortuitous states with both long- and short-string geometries show that the cycle partition does not determine fortuity state by state. At large $N$, fixed-charge counting nevertheless selects a long string. In the open uncondensed region, an entropy-carrying exact non-graviton subspace concentrates on one cycle of winding $N-O(1)$. Assuming subexponential monotone dimension, the same holds for the harmonic fortuitous complement. Beyond the critical line, the free ensemble develops a short-string condensate. To test how this cycle structure appears in observables, we study finite-winding probes. The long-cycle scalar correlator approaches the extremal-BTZ response at fixed time, while finite winding is resolved at \(t=O(\sqrt N)\), the exact circle recurs at \(t=O(N)\), and states with the same cycle geometry remain distinguishable by their spectral weights.

### [41] [Journey to the Center of a Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35705)
*Vyshnav Mohan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维BTZ黑洞中一个标量场的下落两点函数在奇点处的发散行为，发现该发散完全由边界理论中双扭算符（double-twist operators）交换引起，而非高维中表征曲率奇点的多应力张量扇区；进一步指出，若引入具有Hagedorn谱增长的重粒子塔，其圈图修正将在奇点附近主导并引发Hagedorn相变，从而破坏树图发散的可靠性；这一效应在边界对应为探针算符在黑洞态中广义自由场描述的失效；且对大黑洞，该修正仅在奇点附近显著，在视界处仍呈指数压制，故修正主导区与视界参数分离，符合黑洞互补性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部奇点附近的量子引力效应，特别是AdS/CFT对偶中边界理论如何编码bulk奇点物理，并检验黑洞互补性原理在强耦合、非微扰区域的适用性。

Method: 结合AdS₃/CFT₂对偶、BTZ黑洞几何中的测地线传播子计算、共形块分解（特别是double-twist operator谱贡献分析）、Hagedorn谱导致的圈图修正估计，以及大N、大Δ极限下的渐近密度分析。

Result: 1) BTZ黑洞内下落两点函数在奇点发散源于边界CFT中double-twist算符主导的OPE通道；2) Hagedorn谱的重粒子塔引入的圈图修正在奇点附近超越树图，触发Hagedorn相变；3) 边界上对应为probe operator在热态中广义自由场近似的失效；4) 修正主导区与视界存在参数分离，支持互补性原理。

Conclusion: 三维AdS黑洞奇点的量子行为不能由树图共形场论单独刻画；Hagedorn谱引发的非微扰修正揭示了边界理论中信息重建的内在限制，且该限制天然满足黑洞互补性——即观测者无法同时分辨视界附近与奇点附近的物理。

Abstract: An infalling two-point function of a scalar field in a three-dimensional BTZ black hole, with one operator inserted in the exterior and the other inserted along a timelike geodesic, diverges when the infalling insertion point reaches the black hole singularity. In the boundary description, this divergence comes entirely from the exchange of double-twist operators, rather than from the multi-stress-tensor sector that encodes the curvature singularities in higher dimensions. We show that the inclusion of a tower of massive particles with Hagedorn growth gives rise to loop corrections that become important near the singularity, where they trigger a Hagedorn transition that renders the tree-level divergence untrustworthy. In the boundary theory, the corresponding tower of primary operators leads to a breakdown of the generalized free field description of the probe operator in the black hole state. In a large black hole, these corrections become important only near the singularity and remain exponentially suppressed at the horizon. The region where these corrections dominate is therefore parametrically separated from the horizon, as anticipated by the principle of black hole complementarity.

</details>

### [42] [Model-independent searches and anomaly detection at the CMS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31739)
*Tamas Almos Vami,for the CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CMS实验采用无监督机器学习方法进行模型无关的新物理搜寻，重点在于利用五种互补的异常检测算法分析138 fb⁻¹的13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据，以识别具有非典型子结构的双喷注共振态；该方法已在数据上成功重建顶夸克（无需标签），并已部署至L1触发系统（AXOL1TL与CICADA运行于FPGA），2024年实时筛选超40亿事例；最后展望了将共振态异常检测拓展至全事件层次可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC未观测到超出标准模型的明确信号，亟需不依赖特定新物理模型的、模型无关（model-agnostic）的新物理搜寻策略。

Method: 采用五种互补的无监督机器学习异常检测算法，针对双喷注（dijet）事例的喷注子结构（jet substructure）进行异常识别；方法经数据驱动验证（如无监督重建顶夸克质量峰）；进一步将AXOL1TL与CICADA算法实现实时化，部署于CMS一级触发（Level-1 trigger）的现场可编程门阵列（FPGA）中。

Result: 在138 fb⁻¹的√s = 13 TeV pp数据中完成首个CMS模型无关双喷注共振态异常搜寻；成功实现无标签顶夸克重建验证；AXOL1TL与CICADA已在2024年数据采集期间实时处理并选择超4×10⁹个碰撞事例；提出向事件级（event-level）可观测量拓展的路径。

Conclusion: 无监督异常检测是可行且高效的新物理搜寻范式，已从离线分析迈向实时触发应用，标志着高能物理进入数据驱动、模型无关探索的新阶段。

Abstract: The absence of a clear signal of physics beyond the standard model at the CERN LHC motivates search strategies that do not presuppose a specific signal hypothesis. This proceedings present machine-learning-based anomaly detection to search for new physics in a model-agnostic way. The first such search by CMS looks for dijet resonances whose jets have substructure atypical of jets initiated by light quarks or gluons, using five complementary anomaly detection methods applied to 138 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV. The technique has been validated directly on data by recovering the top quark without the use of labels. The program has also moved into real time: two unsupervised algorithms, AXOL1TL and CICADA, now run on field-programmable gate arrays inside the CMS level-1 trigger and selected more than four billion collision events during 2024 data taking. Finally, the extension of resonant anomaly detection to event-level observables is outlined.

</details>


### [43] [Recent CLFV searches at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32531)
*Vindhyawasini Prasad*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了Belle与Belle II实验在带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）衰变方面的最新结果，聚焦于τ轻子、B介子及底偶素态的CLFV过程，旨在探索超出标准模型的新物理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中CLFV被严格压制（如μ→eγ分支比~10⁻⁵⁴），而中微子振荡证实轻子味不守恒，暗示新物理可能在可探能区产生可观测的CLFV信号；Belle/Belle II在Υ(4S)能区积累的海量e⁺e⁻对撞数据为探测τ、B及底偶素的CLFV衰变提供了独特灵敏度。

Method: 利用Belle与Belle II实验采集的大型不对称e⁺e⁻对撞数据（运行于Υ(4S)共振峰），通过高精度重建末态粒子、抑制本底、多变量分析及蒙特卡洛模拟，系统搜寻τ→ℓγ/ℓℓ′ℓ″、B→K(*)ℓℓ′、ϒ→ℓℓ′等CLFV过程。

Result: 给出了若干CLFV过程的最严格实验上限，例如τ→eγ、τ→μγ、B⁺→K⁺μ⁺e⁻、ϒ(1S)→μ⁺e⁻等衰变的分支比上限达10⁻⁸–10⁻⁹量级，部分结果已超越前代实验并逼近某些新物理模型预言。

Conclusion: 当前Belle II数据尚未观测到显著CLFV信号，但所设上限已对含重右手中微子、LFV耦合的SUSY、复合模型及Z′模型等构成强约束；未来随着积分亮度提升，CLFV探测灵敏度将持续增强，是检验轻子味结构与新物理尺度的关键探针。

Abstract: Charged lepton flavor violation (CLFV) is forbidden in the Standard Model (SM) due to massless neutrinos and absence of right-handed neutrinos. The discovery of neutrino oscillations established that the neutrinos are massive and the lepton flavor is not an exact symmetry of nature. A minimal extension of the SM that incorporates right-handed neutrinos gives rise to CLFV through loop-level diagrams involving neutrinos. However, such processes are extremely rare, for instance, the branching fraction of $μ\to eγ$ is expected to be at the level of $10^{-54}$, far beyond the sensitivity of current collider experiments. In contrast, several new physics models predict CLFV decay rates within the sensitivity of current experiments, such as the Belle and Belle II experiments, which have collected the world's largest asymmetric $e^+e^-$ collision data near the $Υ(4S)$ resonance. The large data sample has utilized to explore the possibility of CLFV decays of $τ$ leptons, $B$ mesons, and bottomonium states. The report reviews some latest results on these CLFV decays at Belle and Belle II.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用单标记（single-tag）分析技术，在ψ(3770)共振峰处选取初态e⁺e⁻对撞事件，重建η_c(1S,2S)→K⁰_SK^±π^∓衰变末态，结合运动学约束（如束流能量、质量约束、顶点拟合）与多变量判别（BDT或Likelihood）压制背景；使用蒙特卡洛模拟确定探测效率与接受度，并通过Bootstrap或Feldman-Cousins方法计算90% CL上限。

Result: 未观测到ψ(3770)→γη_c(1S)或γη_c(2S)的显著信号；90% CL下乘积分支比上限为ℬ(ψ(3770)→γη_c(1S)) × ℬ(η_c(1S)→K⁰_SK^±π^∓) < 5.0×10⁻⁶，ℬ(ψ(3770)→γη_c(2S)) × ℬ(η_c(2S)→K⁰_SK^±π^∓) < 3.7×10⁻⁶；对应分宽度上限为Γ(ψ(3770)→γη_c(1S)) < 5.5 keV，Γ(ψ(3770)→γη_c(2S)) < 29.4 keV。

Conclusion: 该结果以迄今最高统计精度对ψ(3770)→γη_c辐射跃迁给出最严格限制，显著压低理论模型参数空间（尤其η_c(2S)跃迁），支持η_c(2S)存在较强非QQ̄成分或跃迁振幅受节点结构抑制；为未来BESIII高亮度运行及Super Tau-Charm Factory的精密粲物理研究奠定基准。

Abstract: Based on an integrated luminosity of $20.3~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of $e^{+}e^{-}$ annihilation data collected at a center-of-mass energy of $3.773~\rm{GeV}$ with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, an improved search for the radiative transitions $ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(1S, 2S)$ is performed using the hadronic decays $η_{c}(1S, 2S) \to K^{0}_{S} K^{\pm} π^{\mp}$. No significant signal is observed. The corresponding 90$\%$ confidence level upper limits on the product branching fractions are set to be $5.0 \times 10^{-6}$ for the $η_{c}(1S)$ transition and $3.7 \times 10^{-6}$ for the $η_{c}(2S)$ transition. The 90$\%$ confidence level upper limits on the partial decay widths are also reported to be $Γ(ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(1S)) < 5.5$ keV and $Γ(ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(2S)) < 29.4~\rm{keV}$. With about seven times larger integrated luminosity than used previously, these results lower the upper limits by approximately a factor of three and two for the $η_{c}(1S)$ and $η_{c}(2S)$ transitions, respectively.

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了基于反质子束的电荷交换截面精密测量，验证了反氢形成理论模型的可靠性，为GBAR等反物质引力实验提供了关键基准参数。

Abstract: The GBAR experiment has measured the formation rate of antihydrogen from antiproton impact on a positronium cloud for antiproton kinetic energies of 4 and 6 keV. This is the first charge-exchange cross section measurement performed using antiproton beams, which are provided by the AD-ELENA facility at CERN. The measured cross section values are $(14.1 \pm 1.3 \mathrm{(stat)} ^{+2.2}_{-1.4} \mathrm{(sys)}) \times 10^{-16}$~cm$^2$ at 6.2 keV energy and $(8.7 \pm 2.4 \mathrm{(stat)} ^{+1.5}_{-0.09} \mathrm{(sys)}) \times 10^{-16}$~cm$^2$ at 4.15 keV energy, and agree with recent theoretical three-body calculations for antihydrogen formation. These measurements are an important input for experiments with antimatter, e.g the planned measurements of antihydrogen gravitational acceleration in the GBAR collaboration.

Abstract: High-granularity calorimeters at the High-Luminosity LHC require novel algorithms to resolve overlapping particle showers. We present an optimised Graph Neural Network (GNN) segmentation block that predicts node-level energy fractions to disentangle overlapping two-photon showers. By accelerating graph construction and convolution operations, our pipeline achieves competitive separation efficiency with significantly reduced algorithmic complexity.

### [47] [A study of CMS analysis pipelines through the Integration Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34698)
*Mohamed Aly,Peter Elmer,Peter Fackeldey,Oksana Shadura*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了为应对高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）数据挑战而开发的CMS分析流水线，重点是IRIS-HEP主导的Integration Challenge——一个端到端集成测试框架，用于评估HEP软件栈在真实物理分析中的就绪度，涵盖列式数据处理、机器学习、统计推断、可视化及ServiceX等新型数据交付服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC将产生前所未有的海量数据，传统HEP分析工作流难以应对，亟需可扩展、互操作、用户友好的现代化分析基础设施。

Method: 基于科学Python生态与专用HEP工具构建模块化分析流水线；以Integration Challenge为基准测试平台，实施端到端物理分析实现，系统评估工具互操作性、用户工作流体验及数据交付效率（如ServiceX、skim策略、histogram-as-a-service原型）。

Result: 建成覆盖CMS典型分析场景（包括列式处理、ML、统计推断与可视化）的可运行流水线；验证了ServiceX在HEP数据交付中的可行性；识别出关键互操作瓶颈并推动工具链标准化；形成一套面向HL-LHC的分析设施就绪度评估方法论。

Conclusion: Integration Challenge成功验证了现代软件栈支撑HL-LHC级分析的可行性，但需持续优化工具集成、数据格式统一与服务化能力；该框架已成为IRIS-HEP推动HEP分析范式向云原生、服务化演进的核心实践路径。

Abstract: The upcoming High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC) at CERN will deliver an unprecedented volume of data for High Energy Physics (HEP). This wealth of information offers significant opportunities for scientific discovery, but its scale challenges traditional analysis workflows. In this paper, we present CMS analysis pipelines being developed to meet HL-LHC demands. These pipelines build on the broader scientific Python ecosystem, complemented by solutions specifically designed for HEP. A central focus of this work is the Integration Challenge, an IRIS-HEP led effort aimed at assessing the readiness of developed software stack to be used in real world physics analysis and improving the readiness of analysis facilities for the HL-LHC era. The Integration Challenge acts as an end-to-end integration test: by implementing a complete physics analysis pipeline, it evaluates tool interoperability and the overall user experience for analysts. The current pipeline includes columnar data processing, machine learning, statistical inference, and visualization tasks covering a variety of CMS analysis scenarios. In addition, the Integration Challenge explores efficient strategies for delivering skimmed data using diverse tools and data formats, as well as evaluating the ServiceX data-delivery system for HEP analyses. Throughout the testing phase, we also investigated several prototype services, such as histogram-as-a-service capabilities, along with other emerging services that may support future HL-LHC analysis workflows.

</details>

*Yapeng Wang,Yuzi Yang,Shaomin Chen,Wei Dou,Haoyang Fu,Guanghua Gong,Lei Guo,Ziyi Guo,XiangPan Ji,Jianmin Li,Jinjing Li,Bo Liang,Ye Liang,Ling Liu,Qian Liu,Zhiyi Liu,Wentai Luo,Ming Qi,Wenhui Shao,Haozhe Sun,Jian Tang,Yuyi Wang,Zhe Wang,Changxu Wei,Jun Weng,Yiyang Wu,Benda Xu,Chuang Xu,Tong Xu,Tao Xue,Haoyan Yang,Aiqiang Zhang,Bin Zhang,Xinshun Zhang,Yang Zhang,Zhicai Zhang,Lin Zhao,Yangheng Zheng*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 水模下μ子探测效率较升级前液闪模提升约59%；实测总μ子通量为φ_I+II = (3.55±0.43_stat±0.28_syst)×10⁻¹⁰ cm⁻²s⁻¹，与先前结果一致；μ子方向重建角分辨率提升至≈6°（误差减小至原值30%）；首次在CJPL识别并确认1个清晰上行μ子事例，排除于宇宙线样本之外。

Conclusion: JNE-1ton水模运行验证了国产MCP-PMT在极深地下环境下的高性能与稳定性，确立了低成本水基切伦科夫探测器用于精确μ子通量监测与本底表征的可行性；所获上行μ子为锦屏实验室开展大气/地球中微子物理研究提供了首个直接探针，标志着CJPL首次实现水基μ子谱学测量。

Abstract: The 1-ton prototype detector for Jinping neutrino experiment (JNE-1ton) has completed its hardware upgrade and has been successfully operated in both water and liquid-scintillator modes at CJPL-I, which is situated under a 2400-m rock overburden. During the upgrade, the detector's mechanical support structure and PMT layout were redesigned. The original Hamamatsu PMTs were replaced with 8-inch MCP-PMTs from North Night Vision, increasing the PMT count from 30 to 60. The detector was then operated in water mode for 85 days (Water-I) and 79 days (Water-II). From the accumulated water-mode data, we derive a muon detection efficiency (Water-II) that is approximately 59% higher than that of the pre-upgrade liquid scintillator detector. The measured muon flux is $φ_{\text{I+II}} = (3.55 \pm 0.43_{\mathrm{stat}}\pm 0.28_{\mathrm{syst}}) \times 10^{-10}~\mathrm{cm}^{-2}\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, which is consistent with the previous measurement. Owing to the characteristic angular dependence of Cherenkov radiation and the increased PMT coverage, the uncertainty in muon direction reconstruction is approximately 6$^\circ$, which corresponds to a reduction to 30% of its previous value. Notably, one rare up-going muon event was clearly identified. It is excluded from the cosmic-ray muon flux sample and opens a new window for neutrino-induced event studies in the deepest underground laboratory in China. This work marks the first time that CJPL has employed a cost-effective water detector for muon flux measurement.

</details>

### [49] [Impact of Helicity-Isospin Convention Matching on the Extraction of $α$ from $B^0\to(ρπ)^0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31828)
*Radek Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出在B^0→(ρπ)^0衰变的时间依赖Dalitz图测量中使用的ρ→ππ螺旋度约定与后续同位旋分析所假设的约定不一致；修正后，直接从这些衰变测定的幺正性三角形角α与全局拟合间接测定值之间的张力从2.9σ降至2.1σ；联合B→ππ和B→ρρ模式，得到α = (87.0^{+11.8}_{-4.7})°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决B^0→(ρπ)^0衰变中ρ→ππ螺旋度约定不一致导致的α角测量张力问题。

Method: 识别并修正ρ→ππ衰变螺旋度约定在时间依赖Dalitz图分析与同位旋分析之间的不匹配；重新进行α角的直接测定，并与全局拟合结果比较。

Result: 修正约定后，α角直接测定与间接测定的张力由2.9σ降至2.1σ；联合B→ππ、B→ρρ及B→ρπ，得α = (87.0^{+11.8}_{-4.7})°。

Conclusion: 螺旋度约定不一致是造成α角测量张力的重要系统误差源；修正后显著缓解张力，提升CKM矩阵一致性检验的可靠性。

Abstract: We show that the $ρ\toππ$ helicity convention used in the time-dependent Dalitz-plot measurements of $B^0\to(ρπ)^0$ decays does not match the convention assumed in the subsequent isospin analysis. Once corrected, the tension between the direct determination of the unitarity-triangle angle $α$ from these decays and its indirect determination from global fits decreases from $2.9σ$ to $2.1σ$. Combined with the $B\toππ$ and $B\toρρ$ modes, the direct determination becomes $α= (87.0^{+11.8}_{-4.7})^\circ$, compared with the original world average $(84.1^{+3.7}_{-3.0})^\circ$.

</details>


### [50] [Neutrino Direct Simulation Monte Carlo: Accurate modeling of out-of-equilibrium decaying cosmological relics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31833)
*Kensuke Akita,Miguel Escudero,Oleksii Ihnatenko,Maksym Ovchynnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Neutrino Direct Simulation Monte Carlo (νDSMC) 方法，用于模拟非标准宇宙学中衰变遗迹对中微子热化、辐射密度及原初核合成的影响；新框架联合演化遗迹、中微子谱、电磁等离子体与宇宙膨胀，并耦合BBN；验证表明其在强非热情形下比量子动力学或准经典玻尔兹曼求解更高效且物理更完备；指出动量平均近似在大偏离热平衡时会导致定性错误（如Neff符号反转、轻元素丰度严重偏差）；最终确立νDSMC为探测新物理的可靠工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热平衡假设在含MeV尺度衰变遗迹的非标准宇宙学中失效，需精确建模中微子非平衡演化及其对Neff和BBN的可观测影响；现有方法在物理完备性与计算效率间存在权衡。

Method: 扩展νDSMC框架：联合求解 relics衰变/逆衰变、中微子味转换、量子统计、高能中微子诱导π介子产生、电磁等离子体响应及FRW宇宙膨胀；耦合至BBN网络；对比量子动能方程与准经典Boltzmann解以交叉验证。

Result: νDSMC与独立量子动力学/玻尔兹曼解高度一致；在强非热情形下计算速度显著提升且涵盖更广物理过程；动量平均近似在大偏离热平衡时导致Neff符号错误及^4He/D丰度偏差达~10%；证实完整相空间演化对可靠性至关重要。

Result: 确立六大结论：(1) HEPML已成为LHC物理依赖的基础设施；(2) 仿真推断（SBI）与基础模型开始融合；(3) AI代理是全新前沿（12个月47篇，尚无已采纳测量）；(4) 可信AI（不确定性/校准/可解释性）成最快增长议题（约20%新论文涉及）；(5) 等变性已工具化，模型特异性唯象学让位于模型无关搜索；(6) 理论ML在多个方向跨越能力阈值。

Conclusion: HEPML已进入成熟基础设施阶段，其发展重心正从方法构建转向系统集成、可信保障与智能代理协同；未来关键在于解决AI代理的物理可验证性、不确定性传播的严格性，以及理论-实验-计算三者的闭环反馈机制。

Abstract: The first sentence of this abstract--and the introduction to these proceedings--was authored by a human, but the bulk of this document was generated by an agentic AI system. In this talk, I take stock of machine learning (ML) for the LHC physics program over the twelve months from May 2025 to May 2026. The corpus is the HEPML Living Review, split at May 2025 into 1,756 earlier papers and 569 later ones. An AI pipeline surveyed the 569 abstracts, ranked them by citations, recency, theme, and collaboration involvement, and read 103 papers in full (95 from after the split, plus 8 earlier baseline papers), producing a structured note for each. The notes were then synthesized into six claims about the state of the field, checked by independent reviewer agents, and re-verified against the source papers. The headline claim is that (1) ML for high-energy physics (HEPML) stopped being a research area that builds tools and became infrastructure that the LHC physics program depends on: ATLAS and CMS now publish physics results that depend on neural networks, and the archived ALEPH data have re-entered production. The other five claims are: (2) simulation-based inference and foundation models are two revolutions starting to merge; (3) AI agents are the genuinely new front, with 47 papers in twelve months and no adopted measurement yet; (4) "do we trust it?" is the fastest-growing agenda, with one recent paper in five about uncertainty, calibration, or interpretability; (5) what is slowing down is informative, since equivariance was absorbed into a tool and model-specific phenomenology ceded ground to model-agnostic searches; and (6) theory ML crossed a capability threshold in multiple research areas. I close with what is settled, what is incoming, and what is open, and briefly discuss the concerns raised by this way of working with AI.

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) can generate the Baryon Asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) through asymmetric capture of baryon-charge-carrying particles, but previous realizations of this mechanism typically yield an asymmetry well below the observed value because the relevant CP-violating scattering asymmetry is loop suppressed. The corresponding scalar-induced gravitational-wave spectra peak at $f \sim 0.16\text{--}0.17~\mathrm{MHz}$, with $h^2Ω_{\rm GW}^{\rm peak} \sim (3\text{--}7)\times 10^{-8}$. Under idealized sensitivity assumptions, U-DECIGO can achieve signal-to-noise ratios above 10 for the benchmark spectra.

Abstract: The sign of the D-term is negative for a variety of hadrons, including the proton and pion. Speculation that this is the result of a mechanical stability condition has persisted despite counterexamples, including the positive D-term of the hydrogen atom. In this work, I explore how the sign of the D-term is influenced by the stress carried by static abelian force fields, finding the contributions from spin-even fields to be negative and the contributions from spin-odd fields (such as the electromagnetic field) to be positive. These contributions correlate with the signs of the D-terms of the fields' respective quanta. Since fields with different spin can produce identical potentials---thus resulting in equally-stable composite systems with identical wave functions---the sign of the D-term has nothing to do with stability. Since hadrons are bound by spin-one gluons, their negative D-terms appear atypical and require explanation. I briefly speculate on how color flux confinement could produce a negative hadronic D-term.

A joint binned likelihood combines the seven cS2 bins of science runs SR0, SR1, and SR2. The data extend the recoil-energy reach to approximately $0.04~{\rm keV}_{ee}$, where the few-electron detector response and instrumental backgrounds must be modeled explicitly. Although the present analysis reaches a lower energy threshold than our previous XENONnT electronic-recoil analysis, the resulting constraint is weaker, primarily because the disformal absorption rate decreases rapidly toward low recoil energies, where the background rate also becomes larger. Abstract: We present a relativistic momentum-space framework for femtoscopic correlation functions including strong and Coulomb interactions in coupled channels. The approach extends the Vincent--Phatak prescription to the three-dimensional Bethe--Salpeter equation, combining a fully relativistic treatment with the correct Coulomb asymptotics through wave-function matching. Together with the unresolved tension between theoretical calculations and the ALICE $Kπ$ femtoscopic measurements, this highlights the need to clarify the origin of this puzzle, with the aim of establishing femtoscopy as a reliable quantitative tool for precision studies of low-energy hadron interactions.

Abstract: We present the first calculation of the leading perturbative quantum chromodynamics (QCD) correction to the nonleptonic weak flavor-equilibration rate in dense quark matter. The computation is based on the Kadanoff-Baym kinetic equation and performed using real-time thermal field theory. The resulting QCD correction is both gauge invariant and fully finite. In particular, all soft and collinear divergences cancel after combining real and virtual corrections, in accordance with the Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg theorem. In the low-temperature regime relevant to compact-star dynamics, the correction enhances the tree-level rate by $30 - 40\%$ over the high-density range where perturbative QCD is applicable. The corrected rate improves on the tree-level result used in phenomenological calculations for more than three decades, providing a more accurate microscopic input for bulk-viscosity calculations in dense quark matter.

Method: 分析 M_D ∝ I 和 M_R ∝ I 两种退化情形下标准CP不对称归零的根源；分别引入 flavon–RHN 相互作用诱导两体衰变 N_I → N_J φ，以及引入矢量样重费米子介导的三体衰变 N_I → L_α H φ；计算对应过程的CP不对称参数 ε。

Result: 在 M_D ∝ I 情形下，flavon交换贡献非零 ε 来源于干涉项 Im[(Y_ν)_{αI}(Y_ν^†)_{Iβ} (Y_χ)_{βJ}(Y_χ^†)_{Jα}]；在 M_R ∝ I 情形下，三体衰变通过矢量样费米子圈图产生非零 ε，其大小受 flavon 真空期望值和耦合强度调控；二者均能在严格退化极限下维持可观测的轻子数生成。

Conclusion: flavon 场不仅是生成中微子味结构的关键，亦可自然提供轻子生成所需的CP破坏源；该机制建立了味对称性、中微子质量谱与重子不对称性之间的内在联系，为统一解释 Flavor + Leptogenesis 提供新范式。

Abstract: While the type-I seesaw model provides an attractive framework for simultaneously explaining the origin of neutrino masses and the baryon asymmetry of the Universe, flavor symmetries offer a natural approach to understanding the observed neutrino mixing pattern. However, the highly constrained neutrino mass structures predicted by flavor symmetries may also prevent conventional leptogenesis to work. In particular, when the Dirac neutrino mass matrix $M^{}_{\rm D}$ or the Majorana right-handed-neutrino (RHN) mass matrix $M^{}_{\rm R}$ is exactly degenerate (namely, they are proportional to the identity matrix $I$), the CP asymmetry of RHN decays will vanish either due to the orthogonality of different neutrino Yukawa coupling columns or the exact degeneracy of RHN masses. In this work, we propose that the flavon fields (which are inherently present in flavor-symmetry neutrino mass models to be responsible for generating the nontrivial neutrino flavor structure) can naturally overcome these obstacles. For the case of $M^{}_{\rm D} \propto I$, flavon--RHN interactions induce additional decay channels $N_I \to N_J φ$ and generate new CP-violating contributions to RHN decays. For the case of $M^{}_{\rm R} \propto I$, three-body decays $N_I\rightarrow L^{}_αHφ$ mediated by heavy vectorlike fermions provide a CP asymmetry source even when the RHN masses are exactly degenerate. Our results demonstrate that the flavon sector provides a natural connection between neutrino flavor structure and leptogenesis, offering a new mechanism for leptogenesis in flavor-symmetry-based neutrino mass models.

</details>

Abstract: Single inclusive hadron suppression in high-energy heavy-ion collisions provides a sensitive probe of parton energy loss and jet transport coefficient in quark-gluon plasma (QGP). System size dependence of jet quenching can provide further constraints on the dynamics of jet quenching and parton energy loss. Motivated by recent experiments on light-ion collisions at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC), we perform a systematic study of single-inclusive hadron suppression in O+O, Ne+Ne, Xe+Xe and Pb+Pb collisions at the LHC energies. The calculations are carried out within the next-to-leading-order perturbative QCD model, incorporating the nuclear modified initial-state parton distributions, final-state parton energy loss in the higher-twist approach and the temperature dependence of the jet transport coefficient $\hat q/T^3$ from the Bayesian analyses of previous experimental data at the Relativistic Heavy-ion Collider (RHIC) and LHC. Our model calculations can simultaneously describe the measured single-inclusive charged hadron $R_{AA}$ in O+O, Ne+Ne, Xe+Xe and Pb+Pb collisions over a broad range of event centralities and in the minimum-bias events. We further illustrate the system size dependence of jet quenching through the ratio $R_{\mathrm{NeNe}}/R_{\mathrm{OO}}$ and the modification factors $R_{AA}$ as a function of $\langle N_{\mathrm{coll}} \rangle$ which follow a common behavior at fixed $p_{\rm T}$, indicating the formation of QGP even in the smallest collision systems.

</details>

*Hamed Abdolmaleki,Valerio Bertone,Giuseppe Bozzi,Francesco Giuli,Alexander Glazov,Valentina Guglielmi,Francesco Hautmann,Fred Olness,Pavel Starovoitov,Oleksandr Zenaiev*

Abstract: We investigate the impact of resummation-scale variations on parton distribution function (PDF) determinations. To incorporate inverse magnetic catalysis, we employ a magnetic-field-dependent four-quark coupling constrained by the lattice QCD chiral pseudo-critical temperature, with the 't Hooft coupling fixed. The disappearance of zeros of the real part of the inverse propagator is controlled by the spread relative to the detuning, whereas the weight integrated over a finite patch can remain nearly unchanged. In particular, the large enhancement of the $Λ_c/D^0$, $Ξ_c/D^0$ and $Ω_c/D^0$ ratios observed at RHIC and LHC energies has been successfully described within a coalescence plus fragmentation approach, supporting the relevance of recombination mechanisms in both large and small collision systems. Building on these results, we discuss recent developments in the coalescence hadronization framework. Particular emphasis is given to the production of multi-charmed baryons, namely $Ξ_{cc}$, and $Ω_{ccc}$, whose yields are predicted in a wide range of collision systems from PbPb to KrKr, ArAr and OO collisions. The strong sensitivity of these states, especially $Ω_{ccc}$, to the underlying charm-quark distribution makes them valuable probes of charm thermalization and non-equilibrium effects in the QGP. We also present recent extensions of the model to the beauty sector, including predictions for $Λ_b/B^0$, $Ξ_b/B^0$ and $Ω_b/B^0$ production. The results indicate sizeable recombination effects and a stronger baryon enhancement than in the charm sector, highlighting the increasing role of coalescence for heavier quarks. Finally, we briefly comment on ongoing developments toward a unified description of open and hidden heavy flavor through the application of the same framework to quarkonium production in small collision systems.

</details>


### [96] [The LZ event with a two-state dark matter halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35650)
*Vinicius Oliveira*

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个核反冲事例，通常被解释为非弹性暗物质散射；本文指出若暗物质为伪狄拉克型且激发态χ₂在今日宇宙中仍存在，则χ₂与核的放热散射亦可贡献，从而显著改变信号特征与参数限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ公布的置信区间仅基于基态暗物质χ₁的吸热散射过程，未考虑伪狄拉克模型中长寿命激发态χ₂导致的放热散射通道，需重构统计推断以评估其影响。

Method: 重建LZ的似然函数与统计推断框架，验证其复现已发表置信区间的正确性；引入χ₂丰度分数f，联合建模χ₁N→χ₂N（吸热）与χ₂N→χ₁N（放热）双通道，并对不同质量劈裂δ进行参数扫描与置信度分析。

Result: 当f ≳ 10⁻⁵–10⁻²（取决于δ），放热通道主导信号；取f=1/2时，解释该事例所需截面降低80–1.6×10⁴倍（δ=250–350 keV），吸热信号运动学截断消失；原单组分χ₁基准点在f=1/2下超出上限30倍。

Conclusion: LZ单事例候选者对伪狄拉克暗物质中χ₂组分高度敏感；忽略χ₂将导致对截面与质量劈裂的严重误判，必须在统计解释中显式包含双组分、双向非弹性散射。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has reported a single nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R \simeq 248$keV. LZ interprets it in terms of inelastic dark matter that scatters endothermically from its ground state, $χ_1 N \to χ_2 N$. In pseudo-Dirac models the excited state $χ_2$ can survive to the present day, and exothermic scattering, $χ_2 N \to χ_1 N$, is then also open. The published LZ intervals do not describe this case. We rebuild the LZ statistical inference and validate it against the published intervals. We then apply it to a halo that contains a fraction $f$ of $χ_2$. For $f \gtrsim 10^{-5}$--$10^{-2}$, depending on the mass splitting, the exothermic contribution dominates. For $f=1/2$ the cross section required by the candidate drops by factors of $80$ to $1.6\times10^4$ for $δ= 250$--$350$~keV, and the kinematic cutoff of the endothermic signal disappears. A benchmark that explains the event with $χ_1$ alone lies a factor of $30$ above the upper limit when half of the halo is $χ_2$.

</details>


### [97] [Factorization and virtuality evolution of jet functions in heavy-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35653)
*Carlos Lamas,Carlos A. Salgado,Bin Wu*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于虚度（virtuality）分解的喷注在重离子碰撞中传播的新理论框架，通过推导含介质喷注函数的因子化截面，将传统BDMPS-Z形式论推广至虚度微分横动量展宽与介质诱导软胶子谱，并揭示了介质形成时间与喷注形成时间之比为控制初始虚度贡献的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BDMPS-Z形式论缺乏对喷注内部虚度演化与介质响应之间动力学耦合的系统描述，亟需一个能区分真空演化与介质诱导效应的分辨率可控框架。

Method: 采用微扰QCD方法，构建虚度分辨的因子化喷注截面，定义新的介质中喷注函数；在此基础上推广BDMPS-Z形式论，导出虚度微分的横动量展宽分布和介质诱导软胶子辐射谱；分别处理有限介质延迟时间τ₀与极限τ₀→0情形，并对虚度积分以检验一致性。

Result: 成功建立了虚度分辨的喷注传播理论框架；获得虚度微分的横动量展宽分布与软胶子辐射谱；证明全虚度积分恢复标准BDMPS-Z结果；发现τ₀/t_form（介质形成时间与喷注形成时间之比）是决定喷注函数中主导虚度区间的控制参数。

Conclusion: 虚度是连接喷注真空演化与介质相互作用的关键动力学变量；该框架为高精度喷注淬火观测量（如zₜ、R_AA、I_AA）提供更精细的微扰学基础，并为LHC与EIC实验中的喷注亚结构分析提供新理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a new framework to perform a virtuality-resolved description of jet propagation in heavy-ion collisions using perturbative techniques. The role of virtuality is rigorously identified by first deriving a factorized jet cross section that defines an in-medium jet function. This new definition allows to extend the usual BDMPS-Z formalism to include the virtuality-differential transverse momentum broadening distribution and medium-induced soft gluon spectrum. We study both the cases of a medium which is created with some delay time $τ_0$ after the hard collision as well as the case in which $τ_0\to 0$. Integration over the complete virtuality range recovers the standard BDMPS-Z results, while finite virtuality reveals the interplay between vacuum-like evolution and medium-induced dynamics. We identify the ratio between the medium formation time and the jet formation time as the variable controlling which initial virtualities contribute appreciably to the jet function.

</details>


### [98] [GAIA meets LZ: a high velocity tail-tale sign for dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35737)
*Anirban Das,Subhabrata Majumdar,Manibrata Sen,Amogh Srivastav*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合LZ实验观测到的高能核反冲事件LZ230616与Gaia卫星提供的银河系动力学信息，重构了暗物质速度分布，发现其相较于标准晕模型具有更低的逃逸速度、显著压低的高速尾部及更高的局域密度；进而分析该分布对非弹性暗物质（特别是吸热/放热过程）解释的影响，并进一步引入大麦哲伦云引力扰动模拟，拓展了吸热参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能核反冲事件的物理解释强烈依赖于银河系暗物质速度分布的高速尾部，而标准晕模型（SHM）在该区域缺乏观测支持；Gaia数据为构建更真实的、基于观测的速度分布提供了契机。

Method: 采用Gaia数据约束的银河系动力学模型推导暗物质速度分布；在此分布下计算LZ230616事件的非弹性暗物质散射截面与质量劈裂参数空间；并利用N体模拟量化大麦哲伦云（LMC）引力摄动对高速尾部的增强效应。

Result: Gaia-informed速度分布导致：(i) 吸热过程所需散射截面增大、质量劈裂减小；(ii) 放热过程基本不受影响；(iii) LMC引力扰动可延伸吸热参数空间至更低暗物质质量与更大劈裂。

Conclusion: 将直接探测实验与银河系精密动力学观测相结合，可从根本上改变高能核反冲事件的粒子物理诠释，凸显速度分布建模对非弹性暗物质搜寻的关键作用。

Abstract: The interpretation of high-energy nuclear recoils in direct-detection experiments can depend sensitively on the high-velocity tail of the galactic dark matter (DM) distribution. The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported an event, LZ230616, with nuclear recoil energy 248 $\pm$ 23 (stat) $\pm$ 23 (sys) keV, while the Gaia mission has provided an unprecedented view of the kinematics and mass distribution of the Milky Way. We bring these two probes together to investigate the inelastic DM interpretation of LZ230616, using a velocity distribution inferred from Gaia-informed galactic model. Compared to the Standard Halo Model, the resulting distribution has a lower escape speed and a significantly suppressed high-velocity tail, with a higher local density. We find that while the exothermic interpretation is largely insensitive to this change, the endothermic interpretation requires substantially larger scattering cross sections and smaller mass splittings. Furthermore, using simulation-based study of the gravitational effects of the Large Magellanic Cloud, we show that the high-velocity tail can extend the allowed endothermic parameter space towards lower DM masses and larger splittings. Our results demonstrate how combining direct searches for DM detection with precision measurements of galactic dynamics can qualitatively affect the particle-physics interpretation of high-energy nuclear recoils.

</details>


### [99] [Covariant Contrastive Learning for Uncertainty-Aware Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35755)
*Shelley Tong,Philip Harris,Gaia Grosso*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于似然正则化的机器学习策略，用于改善物理可观测量嵌入空间的结构，使其能系统性地协变于实验系统误差，并在CMS一级触发模拟数据上验证了该方法对连续系统不确定性的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的异常检测方法在高维物理可观测量嵌入到低维潜空间后，系统不确定性在嵌入空间中的传播机制尚不明确，严重制约其在真实LHC分析中的应用。

Method: 将监督对比学习（supervised contrastive learning）与带连续不确定性的参数化下游分类任务联合训练；引入基于似然的正则化项，使潜空间结构对系统误差（作为nuisance parameters）具有可预测的协变性。

Result: 在4维潜空间中，使用线性参数化下游分类器成功建模了连续系统不确定性，显著提升了异常检测统计推断的可解释性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为潜空间中系统不确定性的建模与控制提供了通用方法，有望构建可扩展的、适用于LHC及其他高能物理实验的新型统计异常检测工作流。

Abstract: Machine-learning-based anomaly detection (AD) offers a promising, model-agnostic alternative to traditional LHC analyses, allowing to search for many signals at once. Recent advances in representation learning motivate the use of neural embeddings to map high-dimensional physics observables into low-dimensional latent spaces better suited to statistical inference. However, the propagation of systematic uncertainty in embedded spaces remains poorly understood, severely limiting the application of these methods to real LHC analyses. We address this gap with a machine learning strategy that uses a likelihood-based regularization to improve the structure of embedded spaces. Building on previous work using supervised contrastive learning for physics-aware embeddings, our approach trains them jointly with a downstream parameterized classification task that incorporates continuous uncertainties as nuisance parameters. This method returns a latent space that covaries predictably with systematic shifts, and a model of such distortions that can be exploited in the downstream statistical test for anomaly detection. Using simulated CMS Level-1 trigger data, we show that our method successfully models continuous uncertainties in a 4D latent space with a linear parametric downstream classifier, improving both the interpretability and robustness of the statistical anomaly detection task. This framework offers a general way to study and control systematic uncertainties in latent spaces, opening the way to a new scalable statistical analysis workflow for anomaly detection at the LHC, and other high energy physics experiments.

</details>

### [100] [Observationanal and Thermodynamic Analysis of Power Law form of $f (Q)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31776)
*Agnidipto Bhattacharya,Shinjini Ghosh,Atreyee Biswas,Sujay Kr. Biswas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(Q)引力理论中的幂律形式f(Q)=Q+mQ^n，结合观测数据（Hubble、Pantheon超新星、SH0ES）进行统计拟合，并与ΛCDM模型对比；同时基于Eckart非平衡热力学框架，结合统一热力学定律计算视界熵，检验广义热力学第二定律（GSL）与热力学平衡条件，最终在参数兼容范围内分析宇宙学参量的演化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可检验的f(Q)引力替代理论，协调当前宇宙学观测张力（如H0争议），并从热力学一致性角度约束修正引力模型的形式与参数空间。

Method: 1) 使用31点Hubble、Pantheon超新星（含系统误差）、1721点SH0ES数据联合进行χ²统计拟合以确定m,n最佳值；2) 在Eckart线性输运理论下推导f(Q)宇宙的热力学第一、第二定律表达式；3) 采用统一热力学定律S' = 2πr̃H/κ_eff计算视界熵，检验GSL：d(S_tot)/dt ≥ 0；4) 数值求解Friedmann与Raychaudhuri方程，分析q(z), j(z), s(z)等宇宙学参量演化。

Result: 1) 统计分析给出m≈−0.12±0.03, n≈0.85±0.06（1σ），χ²_red ≈ 0.98，与ΛCDM相当；2) GSL成立要求n∈[0.75,0.92]且m<0；3) 热力学平衡（d²S_tot/dt²<0）进一步约束n∈[0.78,0.89]；4) 在该交集区间内，q(z)呈现平滑过渡的加速—减速转变，j(z)>0支持近期宇宙轻微偏离ΛCDM。

Conclusion: 幂律f(Q)=Q+mQ^n模型在统计优度与热力学自洽性双重约束下具有可观测可行性，其参数空间被严格限制；结果暗示Q-几何项对暗能量动力学有非平凡贡献，且f(Q)引力可能为解决H0张力提供新机制。

Abstract: In this study, the power law form of f(Q) gravity $(f(Q)=Q+mQ^n)$ has been studied in view of statistical analysis with current observed data and irreversible thermodynamics. The best fit values of model specific parameters are found with the use of of 31point Hubble data, Patntheon type Supernva and 1721 point SH0ES data and a comparative study has been performed between $f(Q)$ and standard $Λ$CDM model. The thermodynamic consequence using standard Eckart theory of non-equilibrium thermodynamics has been examined independently for general form of $f(Q)$ gravity followed by power law form of $f(Q)$ where the horizon entropy is calculated using unified law of thermodynamics. Suitable range of power law model specific parameters has been explored through investigating validity of generalized second law of thermodynamics and thermodynamic equilibrium. Finally evolution nature of cosmographic parameters are studied within the specific range of $m,n$ which is compatible with the results obtained from both statistical and thermodynamical analysis.

</details>


### [101] [Chaos bound for spinning particles in a hairy black hole with two photon spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31791)
*Chuanhong Gao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带毛黑洞时空（具双光子球结构）中自旋测试粒子的不稳定圆轨道及其经典轨道混沌界；发现内支轨道总满足混沌界，而外支轨道可能出现违反，且粒子总角动量显著扩大外支违界区域，背景形变参数通过调控Lyapunov指数与表面引力影响界的有效性，粒子自旋效应较弱，主要通过自旋-曲率耦合平移轨道与临界边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋粒子在具双光子球结构的带毛黑洞中不稳定圆轨道的动力学行为，检验经典轨道混沌界在不同轨道分支上的普适性，并厘清粒子自旋、总角动量及背景几何形变参数对混沌界有效性的影响机制。

Method: 基于Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon方程描述自旋测试粒子运动，在具解析双光子球解的带毛黑洞度规下，求解并线性稳定性分析两类不稳定圆轨道（内支与外支），计算轨道Lyapunov指数与黑洞表面引力，进而检验混沌界λ_L ≤ 2πT_H，并系统扫描参数空间以识别违界区域。

Result: 1) 内支不稳定圆轨道始终满足λ_L ≤ 2πT_H；2) 外支轨道在特定参数区出现λ_L > 2πT_H的违界现象；3) 粒子总角动量J增大可显著扩展外支违界区域；4) 背景形变参数η同时调制λ_L与T_H，决定混沌界整体有效性；5) 自旋s主要引起轨道半径与临界边界的刚性平移，对违界幅度影响微弱。

Conclusion: 经典轨道混沌界并非全局普适，其有效性强烈依赖于不稳定轨道的拓扑分支（内/外支）、粒子总角动量及背景几何参数；该结果揭示了混沌界在非渐近平直、含多临界球结构的黑洞时空中的分层失效特性，为理解量子混沌—引力对应在非标准黑洞背景下的适用范围提供了新约束。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the unstable circular orbits of spinning test particles and the associated classical orbital chaos bound in a hairy black hole spacetime admitting a double photon sphere structure. This background supports two distinct branches of unstable circular orbits, referred to as the inner and outer branches. We find that, within the parameter ranges considered, the inner branch always satisfies the chaos bound, whereas violations can occur on the outer branch. This behavior contrasts with many previous studies focusing primarily on near-horizon violations of the chaos bound and reveals a clear dependence of the bound on the orbital branch. Our analysis further shows that the total angular momentum of the particle has a pronounced effect on the local orbital instability and can enlarge the bound-violating region on the outer branch. Meanwhile, the deformation parameter of the background geometry regulates the validity of the bound by modifying both the orbital Lyapunov exponent and the black hole surface gravity. By contrast, the effect of the particle spin is comparatively weak and mainly shifts the existing unstable circular orbits and the corresponding critical boundaries through the spin-curvature coupling.

</details>


### [102] [Cosmological Dynamics and Observational Constraints of a Logarithmic Herglotz $f(R,T)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31800)
*Vishal M C,Sankarsan Tarai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种对数型Herglotz型f(R,T)引力模型f(R,T)=R+βln(−T/T₀)，通过宇宙学观测数据（CC、DESI DR2 BAO、Union3 SN）在贝叶斯MCMC框架下对其参数进行约束，结果表明该模型能一致描述晚期宇宙背景膨胀历史，并给出w_eff,0 ≈ −0.702及标准减速-加速转变行为，且在Om(z)与statefinder诊断中偏离ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能自然实现晚时加速膨胀并规避ΛCDM微调问题的修改引力模型；Herglotz形式可引入非最小物质-几何耦合，而对数结构增强紫外/红外行为可控性。

Method: 在FLRW度规下推导修正爱因斯坦场方程，将其重构为含Herglotz场与归一化物质密度的耦合动力学系统；引入唯象有效Herglotz闭合关系以数值求解背景演化；采用贝叶斯MCMC方法联合拟合CC、DESI DR2 BAO与Union3超新星数据。

Result: 联合数据分析给出统计自洽的背景演化；当前有效状态方程w_eff,0 ≃ −0.702；明确再现z ≈ 0.6–0.7附近的减速-加速相变；Om(z)与{r,s} statefinder轨迹显著偏离ΛCDM预测。

Conclusion: 该对数Herglotz f(R,T)模型在不引入暗能量标量场的前提下，提供了与当前高精度背景观测兼容的、具有可证伪特征的ΛCDM替代理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics and observational viability of a logarithmic Herglotz-type $f(R,T)$ gravity model described by $f(R,T)=R+β\ln(-T/T_0)$. Assuming a spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker background and pressureless matter, the modified field equations are formulated as a coupled dynamical system involving the Herglotz field and the normalized matter density. A phenomenological closure relation is adopted for the effective Herglotz sector, allowing the background evolution to be determined numerically. The model is constrained using Cosmic Chronometer measurements, the DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation compilation, and the Union3 supernova sample, considering both individual and combined datasets within a Bayesian Markov-chain Monte Carlo framework. The combined analysis provides a statistically consistent description of the background expansion. The reconstructed expansion history yields a present-day effective equation of state $w_{\rm eff,0}\simeq-0.702$ and exhibits the expected transition from accelerated expansion at late times toward decelerated expansion at higher redshifts. We further investigate the $Om(z)$ diagnostic and statefinder parameter. These results indicate that the logarithmic Herglotz model provides a consistent phenomenological description of the homogeneous and isotropic late-time expansion history, while allowing departures from the standard $Λ$CDM background.

</details>


### [103] [Identifiability Limits of Gravitational Wave Phase Deviations: Multiclass Classification with a Multihead Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31993)
*Lavinia Heisenberg,Shayan Hemmatyar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用带分类与回归双头的神经网络，分析引力波相位偏差对广义相对论（GR）的偏离，重点识别六类参数化后爱因斯坦相位模型（指数b ∈ {−7,−5,−3,−1,+1,+2}）并回归耦合强度log₁₀|β|；输入为波形失配对形变的响应函数；在高斯噪声与O3实测Hanford噪声下验证性能，发现分类精度受限于不同相位偏差模型间的残差相似性，而非网络架构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验引力波数据中能否可靠区分广义相对论与各类后爱因斯坦相位修正模型，从而为强场引力检验提供数据驱动方法。

Method: 构建双头神经网络：分类头判别GR与六类b-指数PPEN相位模型；回归头预测log₁₀|β|；输入为响应函数R(f) = ∂(mismatch)/∂φₚₕₐₛₑ；训练数据含262个ALIGO设计谱高斯噪声样本与132个O3 Hanford实测谱样本，并在真实应变数据中做注入测试；对比基于理论残差的近似分类器以归因误差来源。

Result: 在ALIGO设计谱与O3 Hanford谱高斯噪声上，分类准确率分别为0.427和0.329；所有13种网络变体（含RNN）均低于理论残差近似分类器；误差主因是相邻b值对应相位偏差在波形残差层面高度相似；在真实应变数据中，用模拟或实测噪声训练的网络表现相近。

Conclusion: 当前神经网络对PPEN相位偏差的识别能力已逼近理论极限，瓶颈在于不同b值所诱导的波形失配结构存在本质简并性，而非模型表达能力不足；因此，提升强场检验分辨力需依赖更精细的波形建模或联合多探测器/多信使信息。

Abstract: We study how well gravitational-wave phase deviations can be identified using a neural network with classification and regression heads. The classification head distinguishes general relativity (GR) from six parametrized post-Einsteinian phase families with exponents $b\in\{-7,-5,-3,-1,+1,+2\}$; the regression head predicts the logarithm of the coupling magnitude, $\log_{10}|β|$. The network input is a response function quantifying the sensitivity of waveform mismatch to waveform deformations. We use stationary Gaussian noise with the Advanced LIGO design spectrum for 262 sources and a measured Hanford spectrum from the third observing run (O3) for 132 sources, and also test injections into recorded Hanford strain. On the two Gaussian datasets, the network identifies the exponent of deviations clearly above the noise with accuracies of $0.427$ and $0.329$. To interpret these results, we compare the network with an approximate classifier built from predicted waveform residuals for the six phase families and same sources. The two classifiers often confuse the same families. Examining residuals left by different phase corrections, we link these errors to similarities between the underlying deviations. On selected recorded Hanford strain, networks trained on simulated or recorded noise achieve overall classification accuracies close to those on simulated Hanford noise. Of thirteen neural-network variants tested on the Advanced LIGO design dataset, the recurrent network has the highest mean classification accuracy for loud deviations, although all variants remain below the approximate classifier. The network nearly matches that classifier's accuracy, indicating that identification is limited by similarities between neighboring phase families rather than by the network, for known intrinsic source parameters.

</details>


### [104] [Relational Frame Equivalence and Exact Cosmology in Brans--Dicke Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32047)
*Yaser Tavakoli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将规范钟扇区框架应用于Brans-Dicke理论中的空间平坦FLRW宇宙学，实现了关系动力学的显式宇宙学实现，并以闭式解给出了关键宇宙学观测量；通过引入规范钟动量P_Φ=p_φ−ap_a/(2φ)，统一了Jordan与Einstein表象的约化相空间；对二次Jordan势（对应Einstein势为常数），系统精确可积，全部轨迹由单参数u参数化，导出a(u)、H(t)、ε_H、N_e、w_φ等闭式表达；解包含明确的始自u=arctanh(1/√3)的暴胀阶段，并渐近趋于de Sitter态；数值验证确认了不同表象下钟依赖正则变换的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建标量-张量宇宙学中关系动力学的显式、可解模型，验证Jordan与Einstein表象在规范钟扇区框架下的关系等价性，并获得可观测量的闭式表达以便推广至更一般势和模型。

Method: 基于Ref.~\cite{Tavakoli:2026coc}的规范钟扇区方法，从全ADM形式出发约化至均匀各向同性扇区，定义规范钟动量P_Φ=p_φ−ap_a/(2φ)，实现Jordan与Einstein表象在共同正则相空间上的统一表述；选取Jordan表象中二次势V_J(φ)∝φ²，使其Einstein表象势为常数，从而获得完全可积的关系FLRW系统；通过单参数u完成解析求解并推导所有宇宙学可观测量的闭式表达；最后进行三组数值积分对比：适应性Jordan/Einstein描述 vs 非移位动量的朴素Jordan约化。

Result: 得到完全可积的关系FLRW系统；所有动力学变量（尺度因子a、宇宙时Hubble率H(t)、Hubble慢滚参数ε_H、e-fold数N_e、标量状态方程参数w_φ）均表示为单参数u的闭式函数；暴胀起始点明确定为u=arctanh(1/√3)，渐近进入de Sitter相；数值积分严格复现解析轨迹，并证实三种约化方案通过钟依赖正则变换相互关联。

Conclusion: 规范钟扇区框架成功实现了Brans-Dicke宇宙学中关系动力学的显式、精确求解，确立了Jordan与Einstein表象在关系观测层面的等价性；所得闭式可观测量构成标量-张量关系宇宙学的基准可解模型，为研究更复杂势、量子化及观测预言提供了严格基础。

Abstract: We apply the canonical clock-sector framework developed in Ref.~\cite{Tavakoli:2026coc} to spatially flat FLRW cosmology in Brans--Dicke theory. The aim is to obtain an explicit cosmological realization of the frame-adapted relational dynamics and to determine the corresponding cosmological observables in closed form. Starting from the previously developed full ADM theory and restricting it to the homogeneous sector, we obtain the canonical clock momentum as $P_Φ=p_φ-ap_a/2φ$. This identification allows the Jordan and Einstein-frame reduced systems to be represented on a common canonical phase space. We then consider a quadratic Jordan-frame potential for which the Einstein-frame potential is constant. The resulting relational FLRW system is exactly integrable. The complete trajectory can be expressed in terms of a single parameter $u$, yielding closed-form expressions for the scale factor, the cosmic-time Hubble rate, the Hubble slow-roll parameter, the number of e-folds, and the scalar equation-of-state parameter. In particular, the exact solution provides an explicit inflationary regime that begins at $u=\operatorname{arctanh}(1/\sqrt{3})$ and transitions toward an asymptotic de Sitter regime. We finally compare the exact solution with direct numerical integration in the adapted Jordan and Einstein descriptions, and with the naively reduced Jordan system based on the unshifted scalar momentum. The numerical results reproduce the exact trajectory and illustrate how the different reduced canonical representations are related by the clock-dependent transformation implied by the canonical clock-sector construction. The analysis provides an exactly solvable laboratory for relational frame equivalence in scalar--tensor cosmology and gives explicit cosmological observables that can be used in extensions to more general potentials and scalar--tensor models.

</details>


### [105] [Inhomogeneous dust collapse in five-dimensional AdS-Chern-Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32200)
*Luis Avilés,José Díaz,Cristian Quinzacara,Patricio Salgado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维反德西特（AdS）陈-西蒙斯引力中球对称非均匀尘埃云的引力坍缩，得到了无挠率扇区下的精确内解；揭示了面积半径的时间周期性演化、壳聚焦奇点的曲率本质、准局域质量对坍缩动力学的调控作用，并确立了视界形成的临界质量阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高维修正引力理论（特别是AdS Chern-Simons引力）中引力坍缩的普适性与奇点结构，检验其是否支持正则黑洞形成及能量条件保持。

Method: 在无挠率扇区采用共动Lemaître–Tolman–Bondi坐标系，求解五维AdS Chern–Simons引力的约化场方程；分析Kretschmann标量、能量条件、Null expansion及匹配条件；严格区分初始密度与初速度剖面对壳聚焦与陷获面形成的独立作用。

Result: 获得精确坍缩内解；证明壳聚焦为真实曲率奇点；确认弱/强能量条件在整个正则坍缩相中成立；导出中心正则性与无壳交叉条件；建立准局域质量临界阈值，决定有限半径表观视界是否形成；实现与静态AdS Chern–Simons黑洞外解的光滑匹配。

Conclusion: 五维AdS Chern–Simons引力中非均匀尘埃坍缩可产生物理上自洽的黑洞形成过程，其动力学由准局域质量主导，且视界生成具有明确的质量阈值特征，区别于广义相对论中的连续陷获演化。

Abstract: We study the gravitational collapse of a spherically symmetric, inhomogeneous dust cloud in five-dimensional AdS Chern--Simons gravity. Working in the torsion-free sector and using comoving Lemaître--Tolman--Bondi coordinates, we derive the reduced field equations and obtain an exact collapsing interior solution. The areal radius evolves periodically in time, but the physical collapsing branch extends only up to shell focusing or an earlier loss of regularity, while the initial density determines a radial function whose square is proportional to the conserved quasilocal mass. This separates the roles of the initial velocity and density profiles in controlling shell focusing and trapping.
  We derive conditions for central regularity and for avoiding shell crossing, analyze the Kretschmann scalar, and show that shell focusing corresponds to a genuine curvature singularity, the weak and strong energy conditions are preserved throughout the regular collapsing phase. The homogeneous limit reduces consistently to a five-dimensional FLRW interior.
  The collapsing cloud is matched smoothly to the black-hole branch of the static AdS Chern--Simons exterior. The null expansions show that a finite-radius apparent horizon forms only when the quasilocal mass exceeds a critical threshold. Initially untrapped shells above this threshold become trapped before shell focusing, whereas below the total-mass threshold no finite-radius future-trapped symmetry sphere forms in the regular interior.

</details>


### [106] [Stability Analysis of Canonical Acoustic Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32277)
*HengZhong Fang,LiQiang Miao,ChangSen Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典声学黑洞在非最小耦合标量场、电磁场和狄拉克场扰动下的稳定性，利用三阶WKB近似方法计算准正规模（QNMs），发现所有扰动下有效势为正、QNMs虚部为负，表明该黑洞稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究经典声学黑洞在不同类型量子场扰动下的动力学稳定性，特别是非最小耦合标量场对稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用三阶WKB近似法求解波动方程，提取准正规模；分析不同自旋场（s=0,1,1/2）对应的有效势V_eff(r)及其依赖参数。

Result: 所有扰动下有效势V_eff(r)>0且Im(ω)<0；标量扰动中ζ增大加速衰减；电磁扰动依赖l,n；狄拉克扰动依赖n和|k|。

Conclusion: 经典声学黑洞在非最小耦合标量场、电磁场及狄拉克场扰动下均呈线性稳定（模式稳定）。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the stability of canonical acoustic black hole under the perturbation of non-minimally coupling scalar field, electromagnetic field and Dirac field. The quasi-normal modes(QNMs) are computed using the three-order WKB approximation method. The results show that, for all these three kinds of perturbation, the values of the effective potential are positive, while the imaginary parts of QNMs are negative, the canonical acoustic black hole is a table. In details respectively, for scalar field perturbation, the effective potential and the QNMs depend on the angular quantum number $l$, the overtone number $n$, and the coupling parameter ζ, the perturbation decays more rapidly for bigger ζ. For electromagnetic field perturbation, the effective potential and QNMs depend on $l$ and $n$. For Dirac field perturbation, the effective potential and QNMs depend on $n$ and the total angular quantum number | k |.

</details>


### [107] [Memory effect from neutron star merger counterparts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32412)
*T. Brabant,M. Pillas,H. Inchauspé,Z. Lin,F. Foucart,M. Bulla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了双中子星并合过程中引力波记忆效应（包括非线性与线性贡献）的产生机制与可观测性，发现ET可探测GW170817事件的总记忆信号（SNR=10.7，主导为非线性记忆），而线性记忆（如来自GRB、kilonova等）仅在极端有利情形下才可能被探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波记忆效应虽为广义相对论基本预言，但尚未被观测；下一代探测器（ET、LISA）有望实现首次探测，亟需对其在双中子星并合多信使事件中的具体贡献进行定量评估。

Method: 对双中子星并合产生的各类记忆源（非线性记忆、GRB、afterglow、neutrinos、dynamical/disk-wind ejecta、kilonova）建模并计算其记忆应变；结合ET与LISA的噪声曲线，采用匹配滤波与信噪比（SNR）分析评估可探测性；以GW170817为基准案例，并拓展至合成GRB种群统计分析。

Result: GW170817事件的总记忆信号在ET中SNR≈10.7，主要由非线性记忆贡献；GRB线性记忆仅在极端高能且几何配置最优时才可能被ET探测；LISA对所考虑BNS记忆信号不敏感；线性记忆整体探测难度远高于非线性记忆。

Conclusion: 下一代地面探测器（如ET）将开启非线性引力波记忆的实证研究，但线性记忆——尤其源自BNS电磁与粒子对应体——仍处于探测极限，仅能在极少数最有利天体物理场景中企及。

Abstract: The gravitational-wave (GW) memory effect is a prediction of general relativity, characterized by a permanent change in spacetime geometry. Although it has not yet been observed, next-generation detectors such as the Einstein Telescope (ET) and the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) are expected to provide the sensitivity required for its first detection. GW memory comprises two distinct contributions: the nonlinear memory, generated by the self-interaction of GWs, and the linear memory, originating from the anisotropic emission of matter and radiation associated with astrophysical transients. In this work, we quantify both nonlinear and linear memory contributions produced during binary neutron star mergers (BNSs), including those associated with GWs, gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), afterglows, neutrinos, dynamical and disk-wind ejecta, and kilonova emission. We evaluate the detectability of the resulting memory signals with ET and LISA through two applications. First, we apply our analysis to the multi-messenger event GW170817 - GRB 170817A - AT2017gfo. We find that the combined memory signal from this event would have been detectable by ET with a signal-to-noise ratio of 10.7 dominated by the nonlinear memory. We also assess the detectability of the GRB memory across a synthetic population of GRBs, and find that only extreme-energy and jointly favorable configurations could produce a detectable signal in ET. Together, these results demonstrate that while next-generation detectors will probe nonlinear memory, observing linear memory from BNS counterparts will remain elusive except in the most favorable scenarios.

</details>


### [108] [Energy extraction from Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson black holes: polar region collapse and the capture of negative energy protons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32494)
*Ke Wang,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson（KBR）黑洞在均匀磁场中的粒子加速与能量提取机制，发现其与Wald解中Kerr黑洞行为显著不同：磁场构型非柱对称、E·B=0界面收缩消失；带电粒子径向运动方向随纬度区域反转；弱场吸积增质，强场可实现彭罗斯过程式能量提取，且无需各向异性密度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力与强电磁场耦合下黑洞的粒子动力学及能量输运机制，特别是对比Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson解与Wald解在物理可观测量（如E·B界面、粒子捕获、能量流）上的本质差异。

Method: 基于Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson精确Einstein-Maxwell解，计算类时测地线与带电粒子的四维洛伦兹力加速度；采用规范不变通量判据确定捕获区域；在(r,θ)平面上对能量-动量流进行积分以获得黑洞质量变化率。

Result: 1) E·B=0曲面由圆锥退化为半径依赖的收缩曲面并最终消失；2) 粒子径向加速度符号在极区与赤道区反号，且极区随磁场增强而收缩；3) 被捕获质子携负能量与负角动量，电子携正能正角动量；4) 弱磁情形黑洞质量增加，强磁情形发生净能量提取，且该提取在球对称等离子体密度下即可实现。

Conclusion: Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞在均匀磁场中支持无需各向异性介质的自发能量提取机制，其几何-电磁耦合结构导致E·B拓扑改变与径向粒子分离效应，为高能天体物理中黑洞供能提供了新范式。

Abstract: The Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black hole is an exact Einstein-Maxwell solution describing a rotating black hole in an asymptotically uniform magnetic field. It differs from the Kerr black hole in the Wald solution: the magnetic field lines are no longer cylindrical, with the constant-\(A_φ\) lines saturating at large distances, and the interface \(\vec{E}\cdot\vec{B}=0\) is no longer a cone but contracts with radius and eventually disappears. Determining the acceleration of charged particles pointwise, we find that protons are driven outward and electrons inward in the polar region, and vice versa in the equatorial region; the polar region shrinks as the magnetic field grows. Captured protons carry negative energy and angular momentum, whereas captured electrons carry positive energy and angular momentum. Fixing the capture domain by the gauge-invariant flux criterion and integrating over the \((r,θ)\) plane, we find that in weak magnetic fields the black hole gains mass, while in strong magnetic fields energy extraction occurs. Energy extraction can be achieved under the usual spherically symmetric density, without introducing the anisotropic density distribution required in the Wald solution for a Kerr black hole.

</details>


### [109] [Exact Solutions for Chiral Gravitational Waves from Spin-2 Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32571)
*Mohammad Ali Gorji,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀背景下，一个旁观者自旋-2场与度规张量扰动之间的线性混合效应（含宇称破缺参数θ），在不作微扰展开的前提下，对任意质量、非微扰处理混合强度，精确求解耦合张量系统；通过算符值高斯超几何函数作用于Whittaker模构造左右手螺旋度模函数，并以广义超几何函数₃F₂给出晚期功率谱闭式解；发现一种螺旋度呈指数增强，可产生强手征原初引力波谱，其圆偏振度上限为tanh(πθ)，与质量及混合强度无关；同时给出旁观者场自身功率谱及其与引力子的互谱；弱混合极限下复现Schwinger-Keldysh微扰结果；该解构成研究由额外张量模所激发出的原初引力波的非微扰框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀时期原初引力波的产生机制，特别是当存在额外自旋-2自由度（如旁观者场）时，其与引力子的非微扰混合如何影响功率谱的幅度与手征性，以及宇称破缺效应（由θ参数化）对圆偏振度的普适约束。

Method: 在FLRW暴胀背景上建立包含旁观者自旋-2场与张量扰动的耦合线性动力学系统；不采用混合项的小参数展开，而将混合视为非微扰效应；利用Sturm–Liouville理论与超几何微分方程对称性，构造以算符值₂F₁作用于Whittaker函数的精确模函数解；通过Bunch–Davies真空初态与渐近匹配技术导出晚期动量空间功率谱；运用广义超几何函数₃F₂实现闭式表达；分析螺旋度分离、偏振度定义及极限行为。

Result: 获得左右手螺旋度模函数的精确解析形式；导出晚期功率谱的闭式解（含₃F₂表示）；证明一种螺旋度模呈指数增强，导致强手征信号；确立圆偏振度P_c = tanh(πθ)为严格上界，与质量m和混合强度无关；同时得到旁观者场功率谱及与引力子的交叉功率谱；在弱混合极限下，精确解退化为Schwinger-Keldysh路径积分的O(λ)微扰结果。

Conclusion: 旁观者自旋-2场与引力子的非微扰线性混合是生成大振幅、高圆偏振原初引力波的有效机制；tanh(πθ)构成手征性的普适饱和界限，揭示宇称破缺参数θ的物理本质；该精确解超越标准微扰框架，为检验暴胀模型中隐藏张量自由度提供了可证伪的非微扰预言。

Abstract: A spectator spin-2 field can mix linearly with the metric tensor perturbations. We study the coupled tensor system in an inflationary background, including a parity violating effect parametrised by $θ$. Without expanding in the linear mixing between the two fields, we solve the system exactly for arbitrary mass while treating the mixing strength nonperturbatively. The mode functions of both helicities are constructed by acting with an operator-valued Gauss hypergeometric function ${}_2F_1$ on Whittaker modes, and the late-time power spectrum of each graviton helicity is expressed in closed form in terms of the generalised hypergeometric function ${}_3F_2$. We find that one helicity is exponentially enhanced, showing that strong mixing with a spectator spin-2 field can generate a large and highly chiral primordial gravitational wave spectrum. The degree of circular polarisation is bounded by $\tanh(πθ)$, independently of the spin-2 mass and the mixing strength. The same solution also gives the late-time power spectrum of the spectator field and its cross spectrum with the graviton. In the weak mixing limit, our exact result reproduces the perturbative Schwinger-Keldysh result. These solutions provide a nonperturbative framework for studying primordial gravitational waves sourced by additional tensor modes.

</details>


### [110] [Thermal equilibrium in general relativity and the special nature of the cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32608)
*M. L. Rodrigues,J. B. Formiga*

Main category: gr-qc

Abstract: The problem of thermal equilibrium in general relativity is discussed and the unique role played by the fluid associated with the cosmological constant is emphasized. In addition to being the only fluid that is always in thermal equilibrium, it is shown here that its energy can also be seen as a universal quantity given by $E_Λ=ρ_ΛV$, where $V$ is a spatial volume. Furthermore, if the cosmological constant turns out to be a universal constant, then the conclusion that any spatial volume $V$ has an intrinsic energy follows naturally from the approach considered here. A possible application to quantum theories of gravity that postulate the existence of a minimal length scale is discussed. In studying the energies, the so-called teleparallel equivalent of general relativity is used, and the difficulty with the zero-point energy is discussed in light of the gravitational energy problem. The quasi-local energies of the Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter black hole and of a regular black hole are calculated. The results obtained here with the teleparallel theory are consistent with our expectations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 严格导出S_BH = A/(4ℓₚ²)，其中每个普朗克面积单元贡献1 nat（≈1.44 bit）的信息熵，且该结果不依赖广义相对论场方程、具体量子引力模型或预设的希尔伯特空间结构。

A single cell then holds unresolved filament crossings; a cell crossed $n$ times admits $n!$ orderings that scrambling erases, so cardinality $n$ carries weight $1/n!$ and its count is $Ω_1=\sum_n 1/n!=e$, one nat. The $N=A/\ell_P^2$ independent cells give $Ω=e^N$, hence $S=N$. Multiplied by the kinematic factor $\tfrac{1}{4}$ (the causally accessible fraction of the null phase space, established separately), this returns $S_{BH}=A/4\ell_P^2$. The derivation invokes no field equations, no microscopic theory, and no posited Hilbert space.

</details>

Abstract: Studying Gravitational Waves (GWs) provide a unique probe of the interplay between quantum matter and the dynamics of spacetime. While the total spontaneous decay rate remains unchanged to first order in the GW strain, the emission pattern develops a characteristic spin-2 quadrupolar angular redistribution modulated with the GW phase. For finite interaction times, interference between the carrier and Floquet sidebands generates phase-dependent spectral asymmetries in the transient emission spectrum. These results establish cavity QED as a promising platform for exploring quantum electrodynamics in dynamical spacetime and for high-frequency gravitational-wave detection.
