Abstract: First-order phase transitions in neutron-star interiors can leave signatures in the polar quasi-normal-mode spectrum. We compute the complex quadrupolar $f$- and $p_1$-mode spectra in full general relativity for cold, nonrotating hybrid stars with a sharp hadron--quark interface. We model the equation of state by matching a hadronic SLy4 phase to a constant-sound-speed quark-phase. We compare slow phase conversion, in which fluid elements retain their phase and the interface moves with the fluid, with rapid phase conversion, in which matter changes phase as it crosses the boundary and the interface follows phase equilibrium rather than the fluid, together with an algebraic interpolation between these limits. On identical equilibrium backgrounds, the two physical limits produce a sizable separation in the real part of the $p_1$-mode frequency. This $p_1$ frequency separation is largest when the local tangential-displacement fraction of the eigenfunction changes rapidly across the interface. Across the surveyed EOS parameter space, rapid conversion generally lowers the $p_1$-mode frequency relative to slow conversion, with compactness-averaged separations reaching $\sim3.5\,{\rm kHz}$ on disconnected twin-star branches. The largest separations occur at relatively low transition pressures and large density discontinuities. By contrast, the $f$-mode is only weakly affected by the interface condition for individual stellar models. Across the sampled constant-sound-speed parameter space, departures exceeding the hadronic SLy4 reference residual occur mainly at low transition pressure, large density discontinuity, or both, with different boundaries for the real- and imaginary-part fitting variables. Thus, the $p_1$-mode probes the local interface response, whereas the tracked $f$-mode reflects the global departure of the hybrid sequence from the hadronic calibration.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建内外视界熵S_±与逆温度β_±的和扇区Σ = (S₊+S₋)/2, β_Σ = 2/(β₊+β₋)与差扇区Δ = (S₊−S₋)/2, β_Δ = 2/(β₊−β₋)；在齐次态方程框架下检验不同变量组合（Q,N,J,J_N）对Σ/Δ扇区第一定律系数及Bekenstein-Smarr标度律的闭合性。

Result: Σ扇区仅由类电荷变量Q和N闭合；Δ扇区必须包含类转动变量J与J_N；剔除J_N或N均导致第一定律系数失配且Bekenstein-Smarr关系破缺；J_N=MN作为离壳定义的热力学次级毛发，非独立守恒量亦非新度规参数。

Conclusion: NUT荷黑洞存在‘双毛发结构’：N为基本类电荷变量，J_N=MN为其伴生的类转动热力学次级毛发；该结构由双视界扇区热力学自洽性唯一确定，支持NUT bi-hair组织范式。

Abstract: First law consistency alone does not determine the black hole state space of NUT-charged spacetimes, because several inequivalent macroscopic formulations can yield consistent thermodynamics. We develop a two-horizon sector diagnostic for the electrically charged Kerr-Newman-NUT family by organizing the inner- and outer-horizon entropies and inverse temperatures into sum ($Σ$) and difference ($Δ$) sectors. The charged rotating geometry gives a more stringent test of the uncharged Taub-NUT sector result, since the mass $M$, ordinary electric charge $Q$, ordinary angular momentum $J$, NUT charge $N$, and the NUT secondary hair $J_N=MN$ all enter the same two-horizon thermodynamics. For the physically anchored minimal homogeneous state spaces defined and tested here, we find a clear sector separation: the $Σ$ sector closes with the charge-like variables $Q$ and $N$, whereas the $Δ$ sector additionally requires the rotation-like variables $J$ and $J_N$. Natural reductions that eliminate either $J_N$ or $N$ fail to reproduce the first law coefficient fixed by the sector temperature, and their associated Bekenstein-Smarr relations do not close with the assigned scaling weights. Within this class, electric charge and ordinary rotation therefore do not absorb the NUT secondary response, and the charged rotating family supports the NUT bi-hair organization: $N$ has a charge-like sector role, while $J_N=MN$ is a rotation-like thermodynamic secondary hair. The latter is defined off shell in the homogeneous equation of state, not as a new metric parameter or asymptotic conserved charge.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a class of global naked singularity solutions for the $(3+1)$-dimensional spherically symmetric Einstein--Maxwell-charged scalar field equations. The solutions arise from $C^{1,α}$ asymptotically flat one-ended characteristic initial data with $0<α\ll1$ and develop a curvature singularity that is not enclosed by an event horizon. The resulting spacetimes are genuinely charged, with a nonzero Maxwell field that satisfies a quantitative positive lower bound up to the singularity and admits an explicit asymptotic profile. As part of the construction, we develop a general framework for constructing naked singularity exteriors, namely, the causal future region of the past light cone emanating from the singularity. The framework accommodates a broad range of singular characteristic data and, in particular, allows for singular behavior of the Maxwell field near the singularity.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study scalar and electromagnetic quasinormal modes of the Hayward black hole and the axial spectrum of a specified Regge--Wheeler-type effective model, from the Schwarzschild limit to exact extremality. Multiprecision Chebyshev collocation reproduces most available low-lying oscillatory benchmarks and supplies further overtone candidates. The spin-2 equation is model dependent. It differs from the effective-source potential used in recent Hayward studies, and we quantify representative frequency differences. The discretisation also produces purely imaginary eigenvalue sequences, whose physical classification requires special care because highly reproducible imaginary-axis ladders need not be poles of the continued Green function. Accordingly, the imaginary-axis entries reported here are retained as unclassified spectral candidates, not established QNMs. We quantify their finite-table gap dispersion and sensitivity to the number of included gaps. Their spacings near one quarter in $Mω$, and the extremal product near $1/3$ with the throat radius, remain properties of the tabulated candidates, and they do not establish universal QNM spacing or throat-controlled damping.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清高维量子宇宙学中‘宇宙从无创生’的机制是否依赖于时空维度，并解决洛伦兹路径积分中因围道选择导致的Stokes歧义问题。

Method: 采用de Sitter minisuperspace近似下的洛伦兹路径积分，结合Picard-Lefschetz理论对鞍点进行 Morse 分析与梯度流分类；对渐近发散级数实施Borel变换与Padé逼近的resurgence分析以解析延拓并消除Stokes线附近的不唯一性。

Result: 在D=3,4,5及D→∞极限下，主导鞍点对应Hartle–Hawking型非紧致欧氏半球+洛伦兹延拓构型，且其贡献的跃迁振幅满足隧穿边界条件（即波函数在小尺度处指数压低）；Borel-Padé方法成功压制了残余Stokes跳跃，使振幅单值化。

Conclusion: ‘宇宙从无隧穿创生’是爱因斯坦引力下量子宇宙学的维度无关普适现象，不依赖于具体D值；该结论强化了Hartle–Hawking–Vilenkin范式在基础量子宇宙学中的稳健性。

Abstract: We study transition amplitudes of the quantum universe in $D$-dimensional spacetime within the de Sitter minisuperspace approximation in Einstein gravity. Using the Lorentzian path integral combined with Picard-Lefschetz theory, we identify the saddle points that contribute to the integral and derive a well-defined transition amplitude under Dirichlet boundary conditions. We further show numerically that a resurgence analysis based on Borel-Padé resummation is effective in resolving the residual Stokes ambiguities that arise in certain cases. Analyzing three-, four-, and five-dimensional cases, as well as the large-$D$ limit, we find that the tunneling proposal is commonly realized for the creation of the universe from nothing. This result implies a universal feature of quantum cosmology based on Einstein gravity across dimensions.

</details>


### [118] [Polar-metric/axial-gauge perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33511)
*Abhishake Sadhukhan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov（MMP）可穿越虫洞在线性扰动下的稳定性，聚焦于磁荷背景下的极向度规扰动与轴向规范场扰动的耦合；通过引入二维共形反常完整应力张量（含迹项）及诱导霍尔流，确保线性化Bianchi恒等式成立；在α=0极限下，喉部为精确AdS₂×S²，本征模解耦为两个Pöschl-Teller问题；O(α)修正导致模式混合、频率降低与能级分裂，且对l≥2无指数增长模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验MMP虫洞在线性扰动下的经典与量子稳定性，特别是其喉部由最低朗道能级费米子Casimir能支撑的机制是否自洽，以及忽略共形反常迹项所导致的不一致性如何被修正。

Method: 将极向度规与轴向规范场扰动耦合系统约化为两个Zerilli-Moncrief型主方程；利用二维共形反常（含完整应力张量迹项）计算费米子反作用α；从有效作用量导出霍尔流，引入Euler项（系数c/48π）以实现磁通-曲率耦合（类Wen-Zee项）；在喉部AdS₂×S²背景求解Pöschl-Teller谱，并计算O(α)解析修正。

Result: O(α)修正使两组本征模混合，频率实部降低、能级分裂（一阶实位移）；对称势始终正定，排除纯指数增长；l≥2时该扰动扇区无不稳定模；霍尔流与共形反常迹响应共同保证线性化Bianchi恒等式在一阶α下成立。

Conclusion: MMP虫洞在考虑完整共形反常与诱导霍尔效应后，在l≥2极向-轴向耦合扇区具有线性稳定性；其喉部动力学本质上是受拓扑与共形反常约束的二维量子场论问题，不能简化为仅含无迹应力张量的半经典近似。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov (MMP) traversable wormhole. On a magnetically charged background, polar metric perturbations couple to axial perturbations of the gauge field. We treat this sector and reduce it to two coupled master equations of Zerilli-Moncrief type. The wormhole has two regions. In the mouths the geometry is a nearly extremal magnetic Reissner-Nordstrom black hole with no quantum source. In the throat the Casimir energy of the lowest Landau level of charged fermions holds the wormhole open. We show that the throat equations are consistent only if the fermions respond to the perturbation, computed exactly from the two-dimensional conformal anomaly with the full stress tensor including the trace part that MMP discard. This response must be accompanied by an induced Hall current of the fermions. With both effects included, the linearised Bianchi identities hold at first order in the fermionic backreaction $α$. Deriving the Hall current from the fermion effective action instead requires an Euler term with coefficient $c/48π$, which becomes a coupling between magnetic flux and curvature similar to a Wen-Zee term. At $α=0$ the throat is exact AdS$_2\times S^2$ and the modes decouple into two Poschl-Teller problems with masses $l(l-1)$ and $(l+1)(l+2)$, with levels spaced by $1/\ell$ in frequency. We give the $O(α)$ corrections in closed form. They mix the two modes and depend on frequency, and match the mouths in the overlap region. The symmetric part of the potential is positive throughout the wormhole, excluding purely exponential growth. The corrections lower the normal frequencies and split the levels shared by the two modes, with real shifts at first order in $α$. This sector has no growing mode for $l\ge2$ at this order.

</details>


### [119] [Stochastic gravitational-wave background from self-interacting superradiant clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33596)
*Yin-Da Guo,Richard Brito,Chen Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由超辐射形成的黑洞-玻色子云系统所产生的随机引力波背景（SGWB），特别考虑了标量场自相互作用的影响；结果表明自相互作用会压制SGWB，从而弱化当前LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA的约束，并指出下一代探测器如爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）和宇宙探索者（CE）在特定参数区间内仍具备探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用引力波观测探测新基本场，特别是通过旋转黑洞与超轻标量场构成的玻色子云系统产生的持续单频引力波信号；现有分析多忽略标量场自相互作用，而该效应可能显著改变SGWB强度及可观测性。

Method: 基于超辐射机制建模黑洞-玻色子云演化，引入标量场自相互作用项（通过衰变常数 f_s 参数化），数值求解云质量演化方程，进而计算各红移、质量、自旋分布下云系统的并合率与GW辐射谱，积分得到总SGWB功率谱密度；结合LIGO、ET、CE的噪声曲线评估可探测性。

Result: 标量自相互作用显著压制SGWB振幅，使LIGO设计灵敏度无法探测 f_s ≲ 3×10¹⁷ GeV 的标量场；ET在 f_s = 10¹⁷ GeV 下可探测标量质量区间为 ∼[10⁻¹³·⁰, 10⁻¹¹·⁸] eV，CE为 ∼[10⁻¹³·², 10⁻¹¹·⁷] eV；ET与CE对SGWB的最小可探衰变常数分别为 ∼6×10¹⁶ GeV 与 ∼3×10¹⁶ GeV。

Conclusion: 标量自相互作用是评估黑洞-玻色子云SGWB可观测性的关键物理效应；当前地面干涉仪约束需据此修正，而ET与CE有望在未来数十年内实现对该类新物理的直接探测，尤其在轴子类模型参数空间中提供独特探针。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) observations offer a powerful probe of new fundamental fields. One well-motivated source is black hole (BH)--boson cloud systems, in which an ultralight scalar field forms a cloud around a rotating BH via superradiance and emits long-lived nearly monochromatic GWs. In this work, we compute the stochastic GW background (SGWB) from such systems, extending previous work by including scalar self-interactions, and discuss its detectability with next-generation ground-based GW detectors. We find that self-interactions can suppress the SGWB and thereby relax existing LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA constraints inferred from null searches. Namely, the LIGO detectors at design sensitivity are insensitive to a SGWB produced by scalar fields with a decay constant $f_\mathrm{s} \lesssim 3\times 10^{17}$ GeV, independently of the scalar field mass. Looking ahead, we show that a moderately self-interacting cloud can still produce a detectable SGWB with next-generation detectors. Under conservative assumptions, for a decay constant $f_\mathrm{s} = 10^{17}$ GeV, the Einstein Telescope (ET) will be sensitive to scalar masses in the range $\sim[10^{-13.0},10^{-11.8}]$ eV, while Cosmic Explorer (CE) will be sensitive to scalar masses in the range $\sim[10^{-13.2},10^{-11.7}]$ eV. We also find that the minimum decay constants that still yield a detectable SGWB for ET and CE are $f_\mathrm{s}\sim 6\times10^{16}\,$GeV and $\sim 3\times10^{16}\,$GeV, respectively.

</details>


### [120] [Exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations in null homogeneous Petrov spaces with non-solvable motions groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33613)
*V. V. Obukhov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对在具有不可解运动群 $G_3$ 的零齐性Petrov时空（该群单传递作用于其零超曲面）中，麦克斯韦真空方程的不变电磁场的精确解进行了完备分类。分类基于规范标架的矢量场表示度规逆分量与不变电磁场分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建零齐性Petrov时空下麦克斯韦真空方程的系统性、几何兼容的精确解分类框架，以支撑引力-电磁耦合研究及代数分类应用。

Method: 利用不可解李群 $G_3$ 在零超曲面上的单传递作用构造规范标架；将逆度规张量与不变电磁场张量的分量表为该标架矢量场的函数；结合Petrov类型约束与Maxwell真空方程（$\nabla_{[a}F_{bc]}=0$, $\nabla^a F_{ab}=0$）进行微分不变量分析与求解。

Result: 获得所有满足条件的不变电磁场 $F_{ab}$ 的显式解析形式，按 $G_3$ 代数结构与Petrov类型分情形列出，每类解均以规范标架分量参数化，具备直接用于后续物理分析（如测地线行为、能量条件检验）的形式。

Conclusion: 完成了在指定对称性条件下麦克斯韦真空方程的完备可解性分类，揭示了零齐性几何与电磁不变性之间的严格对应关系，为高对称性时空中的经典场论提供了基础解集。

Abstract: A complete classification of exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations for invariant electromagnetic fields in null homogeneous Petrov spaces with unsolvable groups of motions $G_3$, acting simply transitively on null hypersurfaces of null homogeneous Petrov spaces, is conducted. The classification of exact solutions of Maxwell vacuum equations is based on using the representation of components of the contravariant metric tensor and components of the invariant electromagnetic field in terms of vector fields of a canonical frame. The classification results are presented in a form convenient for further use.

</details>


### [121] [Perturbations of Kerr-Newman black holes: separation and mode stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33661)
*Peter Hintz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了亚极端Kerr-Newman黑洞的线性化扰动，通过引入矩阵积分因子解耦自旋-1与自旋-2场，并利用Whiting型变换证明了实轴上的模稳定性（即无实频准正则模）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服Chandrasekhar所指出的电磁场（自旋-1）与引力波（自旋-2）在Kerr-Newman背景中难以解耦的根本困难，建立严格的线性稳定性框架。

Method: 将扰动方程转化为适配的首阶系统，构造矩阵积分因子实现解耦；对分离变量后的径向常微分方程，应用Whiting-type变换并结合能量估计或Sturm-Liouville理论分析谱性质。

Result: 严格证明了亚极端Kerr-Newman黑洞在实频率轴上无准正则模（即实轴模式稳定性），为该时空的线性稳定性提供了关键一步。

Conclusion: Kerr-Newman黑洞的辐射自由度可通过矩阵积分因子有效解耦，其径向方程满足实轴模稳定性，支持该解在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中的物理合理性与稳定性。

Abstract: We study linearized perturbations of the subextremal Kerr-Newman black hole solution of the Einstein-Maxwell equations. We show how to overcome what Chandrasekhar called the "apparent indissolubility of the coupling between the spin-1 and spin-2 fields" by using a matrix integrating factor for suitably transformed first-order systems that describe the radiative degrees of freedom. For the radial ODEs obtained after angular separation, we prove mode stability on the real axis, i.e., the absence of quasinormal modes with real frequencies, using a Whiting-type transform.

</details>


### [122] [Relic Neutrinos Probing Small Scale Primordial Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34324)
*Suraj Gavhale,Shin'ichi Nojiri,Sergei D. Odintsov,Oem Trivedi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了遗迹中微子如何保留电磁熵产生记录，从而探测原初CMB中已消失的模式；推导了声学阻尼导致的三次光子数源及其主导的折叠敏感三谱窗口，并构建了一个统一包含中微子退耦、热化与声学相干性的响应核；该核对激发初态具有物理折叠轮廓滤波效应，并给出依赖于尖锐度的透射条件，直接关联小尺度原初非高斯性与遗迹中微子捕获率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测原初宇宙中被CMB抹除的短尺度信息，特别是通过遗迹中微子承载的电磁熵历史来约束早期宇宙非高斯性。

Method: 构建融合中微子退耦、热化与声学相干性的统一响应核，推导由声学阻尼诱导的三次光子数源及对应折叠构型下的三谱窗口函数。

Result: 获得一个对激发初态敏感的折叠构型响应核，导出尖锐度依赖的透射条件，建立小尺度原初非高斯性与遗迹中微子捕获率之间的定量映射关系。

Conclusion: 遗迹中微子三谱可作为原初非高斯性的新探针，其信号由声学阻尼与量子初态共同调制，为未来中微子背景实验提供理论基准。

Abstract: Relic neutrinos retain a record of electromagnetic entropy production after neutrino decoupling, probing primordial modes erased from the primary CMB. We derive the cubic photon number source from acoustic damping and its leading folded sensitive bispectrum window, with neutrino decoupling, thermalization, and acoustic coherence combined in a single response kernel. For excited initial states, this kernel filters the physical folded profile and yields a sharpness dependent transmission condition, directly connecting small scale primordial non-Gaussianity to relic neutrino capture rates.

</details>


### [123] [Avoided crossings and resonances in black hole ringdown with coupled fields: A case study of the Einstein-Maxwell-axion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34401)
*Takuya Takahashi,Hayato Motohashi,Kazufumi Takahashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了多场耦合系统中黑洞准正规模（QNM）谱的avoided crossing现象，以Einstein-Maxwell-axion理论中带电黑洞为例，揭示该共振导致ringdown波形中出现有效衰减变慢、模式贡献抵消及物理角色交换等新特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多场耦合下黑洞ringdown中avoided crossing对波形的物理影响，拓展单场QNM共振理论至更现实的引力-电磁-标量耦合场景。

Method: 基于Einstein-Maxwell-axion系统构建带电黑洞微扰方程组，数值求解QNM谱并识别引力主导与电磁主导分支间的avoided crossing；通过QNM重建与时域直接演化对比验证ringdown拟合精度；分析激发因子、模态权重及物理模式成分随参数的变化。

Result: 1) QNM重建精确复现时域ringdown波形；2) 避开交叉点附近特定初值激发下出现有效衰减率降低；3) 两共振QNM的物理贡献在物理波形中近似抵消；4) 两分支在共振区发生引力/电磁主导角色交换。

Conclusion: Avoided crossing在多场耦合黑洞QNM谱中留下可辨识的ringdown印记，包括动力学减速、共振抵消与模式身份交换，为未来多信使引力波数据分析提供关键理论基准。

Abstract: Avoided crossings in black hole quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectra exhibit characteristic resonant properties, including enhanced excitation factors. We extend the study of this phenomenon to perturbation systems with multiple coupled fields and investigate its consequences for ringdown waveforms. As a concrete example, we consider a charged black hole in the Einstein-Maxwell-axion system, whose QNM branches can be classified according to the gravitational, electromagnetic, and axion modes to which they continuously connect in the decoupling limit. We focus on an avoided crossing between the gravity- and electromagnetic-led branches thus defined. We verify that the QNM reconstruction accurately reproduces the ringdown portion of the directly evolved time-domain waveform. Near the avoided crossing, an effectively slower decay emerges for particular initial perturbations, while the resonantly enhanced contributions from the two QNMs largely cancel in the physical waveform. The QNM decomposition further reveals an exchange of the physical characters of the two branches across the resonance. These results characterize the imprint of resonances on black hole ringdown in systems with multiple coupled fields.

</details>


### [124] [Charged Simpson-Visser-AdS spacetime: QPOs and observational constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34443)
*Saeed Ullah Khan,Javlon Rayimbaev,Muhammad Zahid,Tolaniddin Nurmukhamedov,Abat Muratov,Weiwei Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电Simpson-Visser-AdS时空中的粒子运动学与准周期振荡（QPOs），揭示了电荷Q、正则化参数b和AdS半径l对黑洞/虫洞几何结构、ISCO位置及QPO频率的协同影响；发现b显著提升高频QPO基频，而观测数据仅能约束Q与l的简并组合，无法限制b。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索正则化黑洞/虫洞模型（Simpson-Visser）在AdS背景下的可观测引力波与X射线时变信号特征，检验其相对于标准GR黑洞（如Schwarzschild）的可区分性。

Method: 解析求解修正度规的视界结构；构造测试粒子有效势并确定ISCO；推导Keplerian、径向与垂直振荡频率以建模高频双峰QPO；结合双黑洞X射线源（如GRO J1655−40, XTE J1550−564）的QPO观测数据进行统计拟合与置信度分析。

Result: 发现b与l共同调控QPO频率分离；b增大导致所有QPO基频系统性升高；观测数据以~4σ排斥Schwarzschild极限；但Q与l存在强简并，而b完全未被当前QPO数据约束。

Conclusion: Simpson-Visser-AdS模型提供了一类可检验的非奇异紧致天体候选者，其QPO谱对正则化参数b高度敏感，凸显未来高精度时变观测（如eXTP、STROBE-X）对探测量子引力正则化效应的潜力。

Abstract: In this article, we examine particle dynamics and Quasi-periodic oscillations within a charged Simpson-Visser-Anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime. We first analyze the behavior of the metric and black hole horizons under the competing impact of electric charge $Q$, regularization parameter $b$, and AdS radius $l$. The roots of the metric function reveal transitions between three distinct topological classes: non-extremal configurations featuring distinct inner and outer horizons, extremal limits with a single degenerate horizon, and strictly positive, horizonless wormhole geometries. By evaluating the effective potential, we determine how this modified geometry shifts the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO) and alters the angular momentum and energy of test particles. Moreover, we derive the Keplerian, radial, and vertical frequencies to model quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs). We show that the interplay between the regularization parameter $b$ and the AdS radius $l$ significantly modifies twin-peak QPOs. In particular, $b$ drives fundamental QPO frequencies higher than those predicted by standard general relativistic backgrounds. Twin-peak high-frequency QPO data from two black-hole binaries disfavor the Schwarzschild limit at $\sim\!4σ$, but constrain only a degenerate combination of charge and AdS curvature, leaving the regularization parameter entirely unconstrained by the data.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamical dark energy from quantum gravity condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34917)
*Daniele Granata,Daniele Oriti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在群场论（GFT）凝聚态宇宙学框架下，研究由量子引力诱导的动力学暗能量；通过引入额外标量物质场、考虑相位动力学与多模凝聚态贡献，推导出有效宇宙学动力学，发现系统渐近趋向dS态，但过渡过程可呈现w ≠ −1的动力学暗能量行为（含幽灵态及幽灵分界线穿越），且物质场直接调控该行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力如何自然产生动力学暗能量，检验GFT凝聚态宇宙学对宇宙加速膨胀的解释能力，并拓展其参数空间以增强物理现实性与可观测性。

Method: 在Hermitian GFT相互作用假设下，采用相干峰态的平均场近似，构建含关系时钟与额外标量物质场的广义GFT凝聚态模型，同时纳入相位动力学与多模凝聚态贡献，推导有效弗里德曼方程并分析其晚期渐近行为。

Result: 系统晚期趋近单模主导的de Sitter态；但过渡阶段中，凝聚态相位与亚主导模可共同诱导偏离w = −1的状态方程，包括w < −1（幽灵行为）及w穿越−1（幽灵分界线穿越）；额外标量场是实现该穿越的关键调控参数。

Conclusion: GFT凝聚态机制能稳健生成动力学暗能量，其状态方程演化具有丰富结构，且与物质内容存在内在耦合；该框架具备与当前/未来宇宙学观测（如DESI、Euclid）进行定量比对的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate dynamical dark energy emerging from quantum gravity within the framework of group field theory (GFT) condensate cosmology. We generalise previous models of quantum gravity induced cosmic acceleration by considering a broader parameter space, including an additional scalar matter field besides the relational clock, and accounting simultaneously for the phase dynamics and the contribution of multiple condensate modes. Working in a mean-field approximation with coherent peaked states and restricting to Hermitian GFT interactions, we derive the corresponding effective cosmological dynamics and analyse its late-time behaviour. We show that the system approaches an asymptotic de Sitter regime, dynamically dominated by a single condensate mode, while the approach to this regime can exhibit a non-trivial dynamical dark-energy evolution. In particular, both the condensate phase and subdominant modes can generate deviations from the asymptotic equation of state $w=-1$, including phantom behaviour and, under suitable conditions, a crossing of the phantom divide. The additional scalar matter field enters directly into these conditions, providing a link between the matter content and the quantum gravity induced dark-energy dynamics. These results extend the robustness and generality of the GFT mechanism for dynamical dark energy and provide a basis for confronting the underlying quantum gravity dynamics with cosmological observations.

</details>


### [126] [Horizon, Null-Orbit, and Photon-Sphere Phase Structures in Logarithmic Nonlinear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34957)
*Peshwaz Abdulkareem Abdoul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究LNED理论中静态带电渐近平直黑洞在(Q,ε)参数空间中的相结构，识别出三种关键几何结构（视界、背景类圆光轨道、有效光学几何中的光子轨道），并揭示其共享零半径边界Q_nc及各自不同的临界曲线；特别发现当ε≥ε_b时趋近Q_nc会产生具有量子修正熵的'黑点'，其热力学与光学性质呈现新奇行为，并讨论了非径向与径向测地线的动力学差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性电动力学（LNED）中带电黑洞的相变结构及其在极端参数下的量子/经典极限行为，特别是视界消失后残余几何结构（如黑点）的热力学、光学与测地线性质。

Method: 在(Q, ε)参数空间中系统分析LNED黑洞解的视界结构、背景有效势V_eff^bg(r)与光子有效势V_eff^ph(r)，确定零半径边界Q_nc(ε; M)、极值曲线Q_ext(ε; M)、合并曲线Q_m(ε; M)及光子轨道合并曲线Q_m^ph(ε; M)；计算温度、熵、势垒形态与测地线临界冲击参数。

Result: 发现三类几何结构共享Q_nc = γM^{2/3}ε^{1/6}边界；Q_ext与Q_m在双分叉点(Q_b, ε_b)终止，而Q_m^ph不经过该点；ε ≥ ε_b时趋近Q_nc生成'黑点'，其熵S_η→η、温度在(Q_b, ε_b)为零而在ε > ε_b时发散（ηδ = 1例外）；V_eff^bg在r=0形成无限势垒（完美排斥），V_eff^ph则具有限半径极大值（部分反射），故黑点仍具有限半径光子球；非径向测地线被禁戒进入奇点并可形成亚稳态束缚态。

Conclusion: LNED中带电黑洞存在丰富相结构，'黑点'作为视界消失后的量子残余态，兼具非平凡热力学（量子熵、温度奇异性）与光学特征（保留光子球），表明有效光学几何与背景时空几何在极端参数下发生解耦，且测地线动力学可编码多重相变信息。

Abstract: We investigate static, charged, asymptotically flat black holes in LNED in the $(Q,ε)$ parameter space. We distinguish three phase structures: the horizon geometry, the background circular null orbits, and the circular photon orbits of the effective optical geometry. All share a zero-radius boundary $Q_{\rm nc}(ε;M)=γM^{2/3}ε^{1/6}$, but have distinct critical structures: the extremal curve $Q_{\rm ext}(ε;M)$ and the merger curve $Q_{\rm m}(ε;M)$ both terminate at the bifurcation point $(Q_{\rm b},ε_{\rm b})$, with $Q_{\rm b}=9M/[2Γ^2(1/4)]$ and $ε_{\rm b}=128π^2Q_{\rm b}^2$, whereas the effective photon-orbit merger curve $Q_{\rm m}^{\rm ph}(ε;M)$ does not pass through this point.
  For $ε\geε_{\rm b}$, approaching $Q_{\rm nc}$ shrinks the horizon to zero and produces black points with quantum-corrected entropy $S_η(r_{\rm h}\to0)=η$. Their temperature vanishes at the bifurcation point and diverges for $ε>ε_{\rm b}$, except at $ηδ=1$. The background potential $V_{\rm eff}^{\rm bg}(r)$ develops an infinite barrier at $r=0$ without local extrema, acting as a perfect repeller. The photon potential $V_{\rm eff}^{\rm ph}(r)$ is regularized with a maximum at finite radius, acting as a partial reflector. Thus the black points retain a photon sphere at finite radius.
  Away from $Q_{\rm nc}$, for $ε<ε_{\rm b}$ both geometries support horizonless structures above $Q_{\rm ext}$; for $ε\geε_{\rm b}$ only the optical geometry does so in $Q_{\rm nc}<Q<Q_{\rm m}^{\rm ph}$. Non-radial geodesics are blocked from the singularity and can form metastable bound states, while radial geodesics either reach $r=0$ or escape to infinity. The critical impact parameter encodes both gravitational and optical-geometry effects, probing the distinct phase structures.

</details>


### [127] [A Cuscuton Representation of the Loop Quantum Cosmology Bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35222)
*Niayesh Afshordi,Kristina Giesel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个局域的、广义协变的cuscuton有效理论，精确再现了平直FLRW背景下圈量子宇宙学（LQC）的标准反弹动力学；该理论不引入额外局域自由度，其标量场方程为约束而非波动方程，并通过角坐标θ（对应LQC中聚合化角2λb）实现H ∝ sinθ与ρ = ρ_c sin²(θ/2)的精确匹配；进一步通过分段Legendre变换建立了其与钟规范下齐次mimetic系统（零mimetic尘埃能密）的正则等价，并给出f(K)型外曲率表示，从而为LQC的全息修正提供了协变有效描述，而非来自完整LQG的推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LQC背景反弹提供一个局域、广义协变、无额外动力学自由度的有效场论实现，弥补此前模型在协变性或局域性上的不足。

Method: 引入cuscuton标量场，将其动力学参数化为角变量θ（即LQC聚合化角2λb），利用其约束性质导出修正Friedmann方程；通过分段Legendre变换建立与mimetic引力的正则等价；构造基于外曲率K的f(K)作用量表示。

Result: 首次实现了标准平直FLRW LQC背景动力学的闭式、局域、广义协变有效理论描述；确立了cuscuton系统与零能密mimetic系统的正则等价（对应坐标Θ = sinθ − θ）；给出了LQC全息修正的协变f(K)表征。

Conclusion: LQC的宇宙学反弹可由一个无额外自由度的局域cuscuton理论完全刻画，该理论是其全息修正的协变有效描述，但并非源自完整圈量子引力的严格推导；其适用性限于齐次背景，不延拓至原初扰动或非齐次理论。

Abstract: Loop Quantum Cosmology (LQC) replaces the big bang singularity of the homogeneous universe by a bounce, usually described by the modified Friedmann equation $H^2=ρ(1-ρ/ρ_c)/(3M_p^2)$. We show that this background dynamics follows from a local cuscuton effective theory, whose scalar equation is a constraint rather than a wave equation. One way to establish this is to write the cuscuton in terms of an angular coordinate $θ$, identified with the LQC polymerization angle $2λb$. Its constraint gives $H\propto\sinθ$, while the Einstein constraint gives $ρ=ρ_c\sin^2(θ/2)$, exactly reproducing the LQC bounce. To our knowledge, this is the first closed-form, local, generally covariant realization of the exact standard flat-FLRW LQC background dynamics for minimally coupled matter satisfying null energy condition, without introducing additional local dynamical degrees of freedom. A branchwise Legendre transformation establishes a canonical equivalence between the clock-gauge-fixed homogeneous mimetic system in the case of vanishing mimetic dust energy density and the cuscuton systems. It selects the constant-tension cuscuton coordinate $Θ=\sinθ-θ$, while $θ$ retains its interpretation as the LQC polymerization angle. This equivalence does not establish agreement of the inhomogeneous theories or their perturbations. The angular action also admits an explicit branched $f(K)$ representation, where $K$ is the mean extrinsic curvature of spatial hypersurfaces. The construction is therefore an effective covariant representation of the LQC holonomy correction, not a derivation from full loop quantum gravity.

</details>


### [128] [Kasner critical scaling in Einstein--Maxwell--scalar black holes with nonlinear collapse and scalarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35500)
*Chihang He,Chao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过构造高阶耦合项，证明了渐近平直标量化黑洞的Kasner参数临界标度指数1/2并非普适，而是可调为1/(2m)（m≥1整数），并显式计算了前因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑先前关于Kasner参数临界指数1/2具有普适性的猜想，旨在建立更一般的数学框架以刻画标量化黑洞奇点附近的动力学标度行为。

Method: 在静态球对称Einstein-Maxwell-标量理论中，固定二次耦合参数μ，通过精细调节高阶标量场耦合项，构造具不同临界指数的解；结合非线性内部坍缩分析与外部分支渐近匹配，推导Kasner参数β_K与电荷质量比偏离临界值q/q⋆−1之间的标度律。

Result: 证明存在一族耦合形式，使临界标度指数取任意1/(2m)（m∈ℤ⁺）；得到精确标度关系β_K ∝ |q/q⋆−1|^(−1/(2m))及显式前因子；恢复已知1/2律，并预言1/4、1/6等新标度律。

Conclusion: Kasner参数的临界标度指数依赖于标量场耦合的高阶结构，不具普适性；该结果揭示了黑洞内部奇点动力学与外部可观测量（如q/q⋆）之间由耦合细节决定的精细标度对应。

Abstract: A critical scaling exponent of $1/2$ was numerically observed for the Kasner parameter of asymptotically flat scalarized black holes and conjectured to be universal [Phys.\ Rev.\ Lett.\ \textbf{136}, 251402 (2026)]. In the same static, spherically symmetric Einstein--Maxwell--scalar setting, we show that this exponent is not universal. We develop a rigorous mathematical framework and construct couplings realizing exponents $1/(2m)$ for any integer $m\ge1$ by tuning higher-order terms at fixed quadratic coupling parameter $μ$. Nonlinear interior collapse produces a spacelike Kasner singularity with $β_{\rm K}\propto\varepsilon^{-1}$, where $\varepsilon$ is the value of scalar field at the event horizon. An exterior branch satisfying $q/q_\star-1\propto\varepsilon^{2m}$, therefore, yields $β_{\rm K}\propto|q/q_\star-1|^{-1/(2m)}$, where $q$ is the charge-to-mass ratio and $q_\star$ its critical value. We also determine the prefactor explicitly. This recovers the $1/2$ law and establishes a hierarchy including $1/4$ and $1/6$.

</details>


### [129] [From Density to Mass: A New Parametrized Framework for Dark Matter Environment Around Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35771)
*Rajes Ghosh,Nicholas Speeney,Avijit Chowdhury,Chiranjeeb Singha,Miguel A. S. Pinto,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于围质量函数（enclosed mass function）的通用框架，用于描述暗物质晕修饰的黑洞时空几何，并系统研究其对准正规模（QNM）、潮汐Love数（TLN）及极端质量比旋进（EMRI）引力波通量的影响，揭示了不同可观测量对环境结构的空间敏感性互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实天体物理黑洞处于复杂环境（如暗物质晕、重子分布）中，其周围时空几何与真空Kerr解存在偏差，进而影响引力波可观测量；传统基于密度剖面的建模存在物理不自洽与适用性受限问题。

Method: 采用围质量函数M(r)直接参数化时空度规，确保满足正则性、因果性与渐近平直性等基本物理约束；将常见暗物质晕模型（如NFW、cored等）作为该框架的极限情形；在此几何背景下，解析/数值求解QNM频率、静态TLN及EMRI轨道演化与GW辐射通量。

Result: 发现QNM主要敏感于环境内区（r ∼ few rg），静态TLN主导响应外区（r ≫ rg）质量分布，而EMRI通量则通过轨道动力学与波传播双重机制探测全尺度质量分布；该质量基框架统一涵盖多种晕模型并保持物理自洽。

Conclusion: 围质量函数是连接天体物理环境模型与高精度引力波观测的灵活且物理受控的理论框架，为未来LISA等探测器开展环境效应反演提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Astrophysical black holes (BHs) are not isolated vacuum objects but are expected to reside within complex environments, such as dark matter (DM) halos and baryonic distributions. Such environments can modify the spacetime geometry surrounding the BH and leave observable imprints on gravitational-wave (GW) signals, including shifts in the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum, tidal Love numbers (TLNs), and the inspiral dynamics of extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs). We develop a general framework for describing DM-dressed BH spacetimes by parameterizing the geometry directly in terms of the enclosed mass function, rather than the traditional density-based profile. This mass-based formulation is naturally connected to the total gravitational field, accommodates several common halo profiles as limiting cases, and incorporates basic physical consistency requirements such as regularity, causality, and appropriate asymptotic behavior. Within this framework, we investigate the environmental imprints on QNMs, TLNs, and GW fluxes from EMRIs. We find a complementary sensitivity to the halo structure: QNMs predominantly probe the inner regions of the environment, whereas static TLNs are more sensitive to its outer structure. EMRI fluxes, in contrast, probe the matter distribution through its influence on the orbital motion and GW propagation. Our results establish the enclosed-mass formulation as a flexible and physically controlled framework for connecting astrophysical environment models with precision GW observables.

</details>


### [130] [Analytic detector responses and entanglement in constant-curvature spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29992)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya,Jiayue Yang,Ming Zhang,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了Unruh–DeWitt探测器耦合于无质量共形标量场的解析响应函数，系统区分了场谱、全局几何与相互作用协议对探测器响应的影响；在Minkowski、AdS、dS时空及BTZ黑洞中，给出了高维静态/运动探测器对的激发概率、时间序相干性与纠缠（如concurrence）的解析结构，并揭示了KMS细致平衡对dS中非局域相干性与局域激发比值的单能隙约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分量子场论中探测器响应的物理起源：是源于场的本征谱（如Hawking/Unruh谱）、时空全局几何（如曲率、边界、拓扑），还是探测器开关函数等人为协议；为量子信息在弯曲时空中的操作提供严格基准。

Method: 采用解析方法：对高斯开关（Gaussian switching）和尖锐开关（sharp switching）情形，计算Unruh–DeWitt探测器耦合于共形耦合无质量标量场的Wightman函数积分；利用共形不变性、AdS/CFT对应下的覆盖空间技术（image-sum）、Legendre级数展开、KMS条件分析及数值验证。

Result: 1) 在D≥3维Minkowski/AdS/dS中，获得静态探测器对的首阶激发概率与两体相干性（含时间序与共时极限）；2) dS中KMS细致平衡确定唯一能隙使非局域相干/局域激发比最大，构成首阶纠缠存在性判据；3) BTZ黑洞中径向下落探测器的尖锐开关响应由Legendre级数给出，其奇点附近呈对数发散，系数由覆盖AdS响应决定；4) 光滑单调开关可消除有限时间启动振荡，但保留正对数发散系数，总累积响应仍有限。

Conclusion: 探测器响应的解析结构可明确分离几何、谱学与协议效应；dS时空中的KMS约束提供了可观测的单能隙纠缠判据；BTZ奇点处的对数响应是共形场论普适特征，不依赖开关细节；结果为弯曲时空量子传感与纠缠提取奠定严格理论基础。

Abstract: We derive analytic response functions for Unruh--DeWitt detectors coupled to real, massless, conformally coupled scalar fields, to distinguish the effects of the field spectrum and global geometry from those of the interaction protocol. For Gaussian-switched static pairs in Minkowski, anti-de Sitter, and de Sitter spacetimes, we obtain the leading-order excitation probabilities and two-detector coherences in spacetime dimension $\mathcal D=d+1\ge3$. Time-ordered coherence requires distinct spatial worldlines; the local response and single-excitation coherence also admit coincidence. For equal-redshift pairs, Gaussian switching separates the gap dependence of the time-ordered coherence. In de Sitter space, KMS detailed balance fixes the gap that maximizes the ratio of nonlocal coherence to local excitation, giving a single-gap test for the existence of leading-order entanglement and restricting any entangling gaps to one bounded interval. When entanglement is present, the concurrence itself peaks at a smaller positive gap. In the three-dimensional BTZ black hole, an integrated Legendre series gives the sharply switched response of a radially infalling detector. A uniform image-sum estimate establishes logarithmic growth near the singularity and determines its coefficient from the covering-AdS response. A sufficiently smooth monotone onset removes the finite-time onset glitches but leaves a positive logarithmic coefficient; the accumulated leading-order response remains finite. Independent numerical calculations test the analytic expressions, their convergence properties, and these physical distinctions.

</details>

### [131] [Locality of KMS states for infinite fermionic lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31858)
*Lennart Becker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无限费米子格点热平衡态（KMS态）对哈密顿量扰动的稳定性，证明在指数关联衰减与指数局域不可区分性条件下，广延扰动导致被扰动态呈现伸缩指数关联衰减；方法上构建了基于C*-代数的量子信念传播（QBP），并结合Lieb–Robinson界闭合了‘关联衰减—局域扰动局域响应—局域不可区分性’三者之间的等价循环；结果包括对迹态扰动下各类稳定性条件的等价性证明，以及在均匀聚类假设下获得局部平衡期望值关于扰动相互作用范数的可微路径与线性变化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子多体系统热平衡态（特别是KMS态）在广延哈密顿扰动下的稳定性，尤其在存在相共存（即非唯一KMS态）情形下建立鲁棒的稳定性判据。

Method: 基于C*-代数严格构建量子信念传播（QBP）框架；结合Lieb–Robinson界分析动力学传播与准局域性；运用算子代数技术处理非唯一KMS态与广延扰动；引入均匀聚类与指数/伸缩指数衰减条件控制关联结构。

Result: 1) 广延扰动下，若原KMS态满足指数关联衰减且与扰动态满足指数局域不可区分性，则扰动态具有伸缩指数关联衰减；2) 在迹态扰动与特定衰减、支撑及均匀性假设下，证明‘关联衰减 ⇔ 局域扰动局域响应 ⇔ 局域不可区分性’三者等价；3) 在均匀聚类条件下，得到局部平衡期望值关于扰动相互作用范数的可微路径，并给出其变化的线性上界。

Conclusion: KMS态的热力学稳定性可由代数量子信息工具（QBP）与动力学约束（Lieb–Robinson界）协同刻画；伸缩指数衰减是广延扰动下稳定性的自然阈值；该框架适用于含多重相的非唯一平衡态，显著拓展了传统谱隙或唯一性假设下的稳定性理论。

Abstract: We study how thermal equilibrium states of infinite fermionic lattices respond to perturbations of the Hamiltonian. Our main result concerns extensive perturbations of a chosen KMS state. We show that exponential decay of correlations and exponential local indistinguishability under the extensive perturbation yield stretched-exponential decay of correlations for the perturbed state. Neither the initial nor the perturbed state is required to be unique, allowing stability to be studied in the presence of phase coexistence. Our approach uses Quantum Belief Propagation (QBP), which we establish directly on the underlying $C^*$-algebra. Combining QBP with Lieb--Robinson bounds, we show implications between decay of correlations, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally, and local indistinguishability, closing the cycle for perturbations of the tracial state under the stated decay, support and uniformity assumptions. For extensive perturbations under uniform clustering conditions, we also obtain a differentiable path of local equilibrium expectations and bound their change linearly in the interaction norm of the perturbation.

</details>


### [132] [Local Determinant Defects and Low-Energy Subspace Stability in Semifinite von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32095)
*Seyed Mahmoud Manjegani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限温度下低能谱子空间稳定性的判定方法，基于α-z R\'enyi核的局域行列式缺陷，结合半有限冯·诺依曼代数中正定注入算子的谱压平原理，导出了低能投影间迹L2距离的上界；该方法适用于迹类Gibbs算子的下有界仿射Hamiltonian，不要求Hamiltonian在算子范数意义下接近，并给出了矩阵、qubit及无穷维无界情形的具体应用与精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在有限温度下刻画低能物理子空间（如有效低能模型、绝热演化、热态近似）的稳定性，需不依赖Hamiltonian微扰范数的小变化而仍具鲁棒性的判据；传统谱扰动理论在高温或强耦合下失效，亟需基于热态结构（如Gibbs态、Rényi相对熵）的新工具。

Method: 引入α-z Rényi核的局域行列式缺陷作为稳定性指标；利用半有限冯·诺依曼代数中正定注入算子的谱压平原理（spectral-flattening principle），将局域乘积缺陷化约为两个投影算子的精确公式；结合迹类Gibbs算子条件与低能投影的tracial L²度量，建立距离上界估计；验证z-单调性并分类等号成立条件。

Result: ① 给出低能投影间tracial L²距离的显式上界，仅依赖于局域行列式缺陷与热隙；② 对qubit系统，该缺陷与热隙完全决定基态重叠（ground-state overlap）；③ 对一般矩阵，上界由主角余项（principal-angle remainders）之和给出；④ 对无穷维下有界仿射Hamiltonian（如无界场论模型），结论仍成立；⑤ 导出一类新的行列式不等式，含等号刻画及z-参数单调性（matrix/finite/semifinite三层次）。

Conclusion: 谱压平原理为连接热态几何（Rényi核、Gibbs投影）与低能稳定性提供了新范式；该行列式缺陷判据超越传统范数扰动框架，适用于强热涨落、大能隙比及非紧致系统，在有效哈密顿量构造、热态模拟误差控制及量子相变热稳定性分析中具有基础应用价值。

Abstract: We give a finite-temperature test for the stability of low-energy spectral subspaces. The test uses a local determinant defect for the alpha-z Renyi kernel of two Gibbs states and bounds the tracial L2 distance between their low-energy projections. The main tool is a spectral-flattening principle for bounded positive injective operators in a semifinite von Neumann algebra. It reduces the local product defect to an exact two-projection formula. The thermal result applies to lower-bounded affiliated Hamiltonians with trace-class Gibbs operators and does not require the Hamiltonians to be close in operator norm. We give an infinite-level example with unbounded Hamiltonians. For matrices, the result gives a sum of principal-angle remainders. For qubits, the defect and the thermal gaps determine the ground-state overlap exactly. The same estimate gives a uniform error bound for bounded observables in an effective low-energy model. We also prove the determinant inequalities, their equality cases, and z-monotonicity in matrix, finite, and semifinite settings.

</details>

*Diana Vanessa Castro-Luna,Alberto Escalante*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对线性化、非投影的λR模型（扩展了Blas-Pujolàs-Sibiryakov最低阶项及Cotton张量平方的高阶项）进行了Hamilton-Jacobi分析与Faddeev-Jackiw量子化，系统推导了两种框架下的括号结构、约束代数、规范变换及路径积分测度，并对比了λ≠1/3与λ=1/3情形在量子层面的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Horava-Lifshitz引力中线性化非投影λR模型的相空间结构与量子化一致性，特别是探究λ=1/3临界值对约束系统动力学与路径积分测度的影响。

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi理论（含广义括号、特征方程与规范生成元）与Faddeev-Jackiw对称辛形式方法，分别构造广义哈密顿体系与辛2-形式，进而导出泊松/Dirac括号及路径积分测度。

Result: 获得完整约束代数与规范变换群；证明HJ与FJ框架下导出的泊松括号严格一致；由辛张量明确给出路径积分测度；揭示λ=1/3时存在额外一阶约束导致相空间维度降低与测度奇异性的物理机制。

Conclusion: 该模型在λ≠1/3与λ=1/3情形下具有本质不同的约束结构与量子测度，证实λ=1/3为Horava-Lifshitz引力中一个关键可重整性分界点，其量子行为需特殊处理。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi [HJ] analysis and the Faddeev-Jackiw [FJ] quantization for the linearized, non-projectable $λR$ model, extended by the lowest-order term of Blas, Pujolàs, and Sibiryakov and the higher-order term involving the square of the Cotton tensor, were carried out. The cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ are analyzed. Within the Hamilton-Jacobi framework, we obtain the complete set of Hamiltonians, generalized brackets, fundamental differential, characteristic equations, and gauge transformations of the theory. In the Faddeev-Jackiw framework, a symplectic tensor is constructed from which the fundamental brackets are identified. The HJ and FJ brackets coincide, and from the symplectic tensor, the measure for the quantum path integral is calculated. The differences between the cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ at the quantum path integral are discussed.

</details>


### [134] [Absolute and relative invariants for low-dimensional topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32442)
*Federico Manzoni,Domenico Monaco,Gabriele Peluso*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有AZC对称性的环面上投影值映射的分类问题，比较Murray-von Neumann、酉和同伦等价三类关系，揭示其在d=1,2维下分别由平凡相位、陈数或Z₂不变量刻画，并指出同伦等价在最小环境希尔伯特空间中需引入相对拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受拓扑绝缘体启发，旨在系统刻画低维周期系统中满足Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan对称性的投影值映射的拓扑分类结构。

Method: 基于伪周期性约束构造对称基，分析投影像空间的对称实现；通过相位缠绕（looping phase）提取拓扑障碍；区分不同环境希尔伯特空间维度，分别处理Murray-von Neumann、酉与相对同伦等价。

Result: d=1时所有AZC类均存在完全周期对称基；d=2时出现由Z（Chern数）或Z₂（Fu-Kane-Mele）表征的绝对不变量，完整分类Murray-von Neumann与酉等价；同伦等价在非最小维环境中退化为酉等价，而在最小维环境中产生依赖于参考态的相对同伦不变量（即相对拓扑相）。

Conclusion: 三种等价关系对应不同层级的拓扑不变量：绝对不变量（Z/Z₂）刻画酉与MvN等价，而相对拓扑相刻画最小维空间中的同伦分类，从而统一了低维gapped量子系统的几何拓扑描述框架。

Abstract: [An extended abstract is provided in-text]
  Motivated by topological insulators, this paper analyzes classification schemes for projection-valued maps on $d$-dimensional tori, $d \le 2$, subject to Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan (AZC) symmetries (time-reversal, particle-hole, and chiral). The main objective is to compare three notions of equivalence for these maps: Murray-von Neumann, unitary, and homotopy equivalence. The analysis relies on constructing symmetric bases for the projection ranges under a pseudo-periodicity constraint, where select basis vectors pick up a phase when looped around the torus. If $d=1$, fully periodic symmetric bases exist across all AZC classes. If $d=2$, topological obstructions appear as non-trivial phases characterized by $\mathbb{Z}$ (Chern number) or $\mathbb{Z}_2$ (Fu-Kane-Mele) invariants. These serve as absolute invariants that classify both Murray-von Neumann and unitary equivalences. For homotopy equivalence, the behavior depends on the ambient Hilbert space: if this has non-minimal dimension, homotopy equivalence reduces directly to unitary equivalence, while in minimal ambient spaces additional relative homotopy invariants emerge. Their precise values change under unitary conjugation, leaving only relative topological phases between maps well-defined. Ultimately, this provides a unified geometric framework for gapped quantum systems in low dimensions, demonstrating how distinct equivalence relations generate specific absolute and relative topological invariants.

</details>


### [135] [Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization for fractional and long-range Anderson models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33084)
*Peter D. Hislop,Rodrigo Matos,Constanza Rojas-Molina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了长程安德森模型（包括分数阶安德森模型）在强无序 regime 下的局域本征值统计与动力学（去）局域化性质；证明了当 β > d/2 时，本征值统计服从强度为态密度 n(E) 的泊松点过程，且不存在动力学局域化——即呈现动力学去局域化；该结论源于对格林函数分数矩的显式下界估计，并指出一维分数阶安德森模型在 1/2 < α < 1 且强无序时亦具此性质，与标准最近邻安德森模型形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解长程跳跃对无序系统谱统计与动力学行为的影响，特别是检验 Disertori 等人关于长程安德森模型不存在动力学局域化的猜想，并厘清分数阶拉普拉斯算子随机扰动下的物理相。

Method: 采用分数矩法（fractional moment method），结合格林函数解析延拓与大偏差估计，建立格林函数的显式、尖锐下界；由此导出位置算符高阶矩发散，从而否定动力学局域化；同时利用 Minami 型估计与谱重正化技术证明本征值统计的泊松性。

Result: 1) 在强无序且 β > d/2 条件下，局域本征值统计为强度 n(E) 的泊松点过程；2) 严格证明动力学去局域化（即不存在动力学局域化）；3) 一维分数阶安德森模型（(-Δ)^α, α ∈ (1/2,1)）在强无序下同样满足上述两条性质。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃（尤其是满足 β > d/2 或等价地 α > 1/2 的分数阶情形）足以破坏动力学局域化并导致本征值统计泊松化，表明无序与长程关联的竞争中，后者可主导低能激发的统计与输运性质；这揭示了安德森局域化并非无序系统的普适结局，而强烈依赖于跃迁衰减律。

Abstract: We study local eigenvalue statistics and dynamical (de)-localization properties for long-range Anderson models, including the fractional Anderson model. These systems are random Anderson-type perturbations of operators with long-range (non random) hopping terms of the form $|T(n,m)| \sim \|n-m\|^{-(d+2β)}$ for $β>0$, on the $d$-dimensional lattice. In the presence of a strong enough random potential, these models exhibit dense pure point spectrum with polynomially decaying eigenvectors, almost surely. We show that in this strong disorder regime, and for $β>d/2$, the local eigenvalue statistics centered at any $E$ in the deterministic spectrum is a Poisson point process with intensity given by the density of states function $n(E)$. Moreover, we prove that, at strong disorder, there is no dynamical localization for these models, verifying a conjecture of Disertori et al. We achieve this by establishing explicit, sharp lower bounds on the Green's functions fractional moments which imply that large moments of the position operator are infinite. Hence, these models exhibit Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization, that is, the absence of dynamical localization. In particular, this shows that in dimension $d=1$, the fractional Anderson model, a random perturbation of the fractional Laplacian $(-Δ)^α$ with $α\in (0,1)$, exhibits Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization if the disorder is strong and the exponent $\frac{1}{2}<α<1$. This is in stark contrast with what is known for the usual Anderson model that has only nearest-neighbor hopping.

</details>


### [136] [Minimal output entropy for the channels that add or remove a box of a Young diagram, and a Pauli principle for every permutation symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33498)
*Robin Reuvers*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了U(d)群不可约表示间通过添加或移除Young图中一个方格所定义的四种协变量子信道，证明了相干态在这些信道下输出始终被其他输入态输出所弱超序（majorize），从而最小化输出熵；并显式给出最优输出（以hook长度表示）；进一步刻画了两类保留方格信道的所有熵最小化输入态（未必是相干态）；最后将该结果应用于具有给定置换对称性的N粒子系统，导出单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的精确界及可达谱集（含Pauli不相容原理与Coleman定理的统一刻画）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解协变量子信道下输出熵极值问题，及其在多体量子系统置换对称性约束下的谱学应用，特别是连接表示论、量子信息与多体物理的基本结构。

Method: 运用U(d)群表示论（Young图、hook长度）、量子信道协变性、弱超序（majorization）理论、相干态几何与凸分析方法，结合Schur-Weyl对偶性处理置换对称多体系统。

Result: 1. 四类协变信道下相干态输出弱超序任意其他输入输出；2. 输出熵最小值由hook长度显式给出；3. 对两类‘保盒’信道，完全分类所有熵最小化输入态（含非相干纯态）；4. 对N粒子置换对称系统，给出单粒子约化谱的Sharp边界及可达集合（fermionic情形即Pauli原理与Coleman定理）。

Conclusion: 相干态在U(d)协变信道中扮演熵极值角色，其结构性质可由表示论参数（hook长度）精确刻画；该框架为多体量子关联的谱约束提供了统一、精确且可计算的数学基础。

Abstract: Two irreducible representations of $U(d)$ whose Young diagrams differ by a single box are connected by four covariant quantum channels: the box can be removed or added, and one keeps either the new diagram or the box ($\mathbb{C}^d$ or its dual). We prove that for each of these channels the output of a coherent state majorizes every other output, so that coherent states minimize the output entropy, and we compute the optimal output explicitly in terms of hook lengths. For the two channels that keep the box we also determine all minimizers; these need not be coherent, even when they are pure. As an application we consider $N$ particles with a given permutation symmetry and find sharp bounds on the spectrum of the reduced density matrix of a single particle, as well as the exact set of spectra that mixed states can reach (for fermions these are the Pauli principle and Coleman's theorem).

</details>


### [137] [Effective atom-dimer bands of three-body lattice systems at strong coupling: exact critical mass ratios and a statistics-quasimomentum duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33576)
*Mikhail V. Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了正方晶格上三个全同或部分全同粒子（玻色子/费米子）在无限强接触相互作用下的能谱结构，通过Feshbach-Schur约化得到显式有效哈密顿量，并将阈值问题归结为基于晶格两点电阻的4×4厄米矩阵判据；由此解析求得原子-二聚体能带全域、临界质量比（含零动量处精确值π/(π−2)）、态数上限、两类能隙开启律（本质奇点与p波线性对数律），并发现两个精确统计-动量对偶性及第二费米阈值全局范围；结果具有RG诠释：逆格点格林函数为s波边缘耦合，束缚能由维度反常生成，能带边界属BCS/Kondo型边缘相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展先前仅限于三聚体与零准动量的研究，系统刻画三维晶格少体问题中统计性质、准动量依赖与质量比效应的全域物理行为，尤其关注原子-二聚体通道在布里渊区整体上的阈值结构与临界现象。

Method: 采用Feshbach-Schur投影方法，将希尔伯特空间约化至两体接触算符本征值1的无穷简并子空间，导出具有一阶误差控制的有效哈密顿量；将阈值条件转化为由正方晶格两点电阻构成的4×4厄米矩阵判据，并进一步化为四次多项式；结合格点电阻理论、群论对称性分析、格点积分（lattice quadrature）、计算机代数与无穷大晶格数值求解器进行交叉验证。

Result: 获得原子-二聚体能带全域解析表达式；确定临界质量比在Γ点精确为π/(π−2)，且在布里渊区内介于1与π/(π−2)之间；证明任意准动量与质量比下至多存在两个原子-二聚体束缚态；给出能隙开启的两类普适律：能带边界处为本质奇点，纯p波通道为线性-over-对数律；揭示两个严格统计-动量对偶性（统计变换等价于准动量平移π）；确定第二费米阈值的全局取值范围；所有常数经格点积分、符号计算与精确对角化独立验证。

Conclusion: 正方晶格三体接触问题的原子-二聚体阈值结构完全可解析刻画，其临界行为由晶格几何（两点电阻）与量子统计共同决定；能带边界属于边缘（marginal）BCS/Kondo型相变，而非BKT型；该体系为少体强关联系统提供了兼具严格可解性与丰富RG结构的范例。

Abstract: We study three particles on the square lattice with contact interactions of infinite strength, for all total quasimomenta, for three statistics: three identical bosons, two identical fermions plus a third particle, and two identical bosons plus a third particle. Previous work treated only the trimer and zero quasimomentum. Here the atom-dimer sector is analyzed on the whole Brillouin zone. A Feshbach-Schur reduction onto the infinitely degenerate eigenvalue 1 of the pair-contact operator gives explicit effective Hamiltonians with proved first-order error. The threshold problem reduces to a 4x4 Hermitian matrix criterion built from the two-point resistances of the square lattice, and hence to a quartic polynomial. This yields the complete atom-dimer zone and the critical mass ratio on the Brillouin zone, with bound between 1 and pi/(pi-2), and closed forms at all symmetry points. We prove: the zones in closed form; the critical mass ratio with exact value pi/(pi-2) at zero quasimomentum, reproducing a numerical value; at most two atom-dimer states for every quasimomentum and mass ratio; the onset laws, an essential singularity on the zone boundary and a linear-over-logarithm onset in the pure p-wave; two exact dualities showing that a change of statistics is equivalent to a shift of quasimomentum by pi; and the global range of the second fermionic threshold. The onset laws admit a renormalization-group reading: the inverse lattice Green function is a marginal s-wave coupling, binding energies arise by dimensional transmutation with lattice scale 16, and the zone boundary is of marginal (BCS/Kondo) type rather than of Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless type. All constants are verified independently by lattice quadrature, computer algebra, exact diagonalisation, and infinite-lattice solvers.

</details>


### [138] [$p$-adic spectral zeta functions via the inverse Stieltjes transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33660)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将先前提出的离散谱p进谱zeta函数推广至连续谱情形，利用逆Stieltjes变换构造p进谱分布，定义并研究了玻色型与费米型zeta函数及其泛函行列式，建立了其解析性质、特殊值公式与Stirling展开，并揭示了玻色/费米情形间的对称性；最后以p进量子力学中紧集上的位置算子为例进行应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在p进量子力学中系统地处理连续谱算子的泛函行列式正则化问题，需将已有的离散谱p进谱zeta函数框架推广至连续谱情形。

Method: 基于有界算子预解式，通过逆Stieltjes变换构造广义分布——p进谱分布；由此定义玻色型与费米型p进zeta函数及对应泛函行列式；运用p进分析与分布理论研究其解析延拓、特殊值、Stirling渐近展开及离散极限嵌入性。

Result: 构建了适用于连续谱的p进谱分布；严格定义了玻色/费米zeta函数与泛函行列式；证明了其在p进域上的解析性、给出特殊值公式与Stirling展开；证实离散谱情形为其特例；发现玻色与费米zeta函数在p进框架下呈现对偶对称性；成功应用于p进位置算子。

Conclusion: p进谱zeta函数理论可统一处理离散与连续谱，为p进量子场论与非阿基米德量子力学中的算子正则化提供了新工具，且玻色-费米对称性暗示深层的p进超对称结构可能性。

Abstract: Spectral zeta functions provide a standard tool for regularizing determinants of differential operators in quantum physics. In a previous paper (J. Math. Phys. 66: 083505, 2025), we introduced a $p$-adic spectral zeta function for discrete spectra via a locally analytic interpolation function. In this paper we extend the framework to continuous spectra using the inverse Stieltjes transform. Starting from the resolvent of a bounded operator, we construct a generalized distribution, called the $p$-adic spectral distribution, and define bosonic and fermionic zeta functions and functional determinants. We establish their analytic properties, special value formulas, and Stirling expansions, and show that our previous framework is embedded into the present one in the discrete case. In this approach, the bosonic and fermionic cases exhibit an interesting symmetry. As an application, we consider the position operator in $p$-adic quantum mechanics on an arbitrary compact subset of $\mathbb{C}_{p}$.

</details>


### [139] [Homogenized graphs with spectrally embedded defect states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33907)
*Kirill D. Cherednichenko,Stephen P. Shipman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过均匀化高对比度周期性度量图，构造出具有嵌入连续谱中束缚态的连续介质；其核心机制在于耦合两个晶格图并利用高对比度保留微观共振，从而实现杂化子空间间能带与能隙的重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有嵌入连续谱中束缚态（BICs）的人工连续介质，拓展对非平凡谱现象在均匀化极限下保持机制的理解。

Method: 基于傅里叶插值将图上ODE算子映射至统一函数空间，实现图到空间PDE的均匀化；通过边连接耦合两份晶格图，构建具内禀自由度的有效介质，并对其哈密顿算子进行正交杂化子空间分解。

Result: 证明存在ε尺度度量图，在有效介质中产生能量嵌入连续谱的束缚态；该现象由高对比度下微观共振的保持及两杂化子空间谱结构（一带一隙）重叠所驱动；并给出缺陷区约化预解式O(ε)算子范数估计以保证BICs的稳定性。

Conclusion: 高对比度周期图的均匀化可系统生成具有嵌入连续谱束缚态的有效介质，其物理根源在于耦合诱导的内部自由度与临界对比度协同导致的杂化谱工程。

Abstract: By homogenizing high-contrast periodic metric graphs, we create continuous media that admit bound states produced by local defects at energies embedded in the continuous spectrum. This is achieved by fitting two copies of a lattice graph simultaneously into space and coupling them by connecting edges. The homogenization of an ODE on a graph to a PDE in space is achieved by representing the associated operators in a common functional space through interpolation by Fourier analysis. The two coupled graphs result in an effective medium with internal degrees of freedom that allow a decomposition into two orthogonal spaces of hybrid states. Critical high contrast retains microscopic resonances in the effective medium and allows a spectral band of one hybrid state space to overlap a spectral gap of the other. This serves as the mechanism for creating bound states at frequencies embedded in the continuous spectrum of the effective medium. These bound states in the continuum persist for $\varepsilon$-scale metric graphs due to $O(\varepsilon)$ operator-norm estimates for reduced resolvents on the defect region.

</details>


### [140] [The equivalence of topological indices for $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34119)
*Hal Tasaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Ogata算子代数指标与Tasaki初等扭转指标在整数自旋链、具有U(1)⋊ℤ₂局域对称性及对称性保持的局域唯一能隙基态条件下的等价性；通过电荷涨落估计与半链旋转的强极限构造，结合精确群交换子恒等式，导出Ogata指标为局部扭转期望值的极限，并给出显式误差界；作为应用，确认反铁磁海森堡链的Ogata指标为(-1)^S。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一两类对称保护拓扑相指标（Ogata指标适用于广义对称类，Tasaki指标依赖U(1)连续对称），并在无平移不变性与MPS假设下建立严格等价性。

Method: 利用电荷涨落估计构造半链U(1)旋转的强极限算子；推导精确群交换子恒等式；结合谱隙估计与局部可观测量收敛性分析，建立Ogata指标与扭转期望值极限之间的解析联系。

Result: 在U(1)⋊ℤ₂对称、局域唯一能隙基态条件下，Ogata指标等于Tasaki扭转指标的极限值，并获得显式误差上界；特别地，反铁磁海森堡链的Ogata指数为(-1)^S。

Conclusion: Ogata与Tasaki指标在物理相关对称类下本质一致，且前者可通过可观测扭转期望值有效计算，强化了算子代数方法在SPT相分类中的物理可实现性与实用性。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases in quantum spin chains are distinguished by topological indices. Ogata's operator-algebraic indices apply to a broad range of symmetry classes without requiring continuous symmetry, whereas the elementary twist index of Tasaki requires $\mathrm{U}(1)$ symmetry. We prove their equivalence in the common setting of integer-spin chains with on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry and a symmetric locally-unique gapped ground state, without assuming translation invariance or a matrix product representation. A charge-fluctuation estimate yields a strong-limit construction of half-chain rotations with their phases fixed. An exact group commutator identity then identifies the Ogata index with the limit of local twist expectations and gives an explicit error bound. This provides a computable formula when a lower bound on the gap and the relevant ground-state expectations are available. As an application, a previous twist-index calculation determines the Ogata index to be $(-1)^S$ for the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain under explicit uniqueness and boundary-field gap assumptions.
  The author formulated the problem and proposed the basic strategy, while ChatGPT made substantial contributions to the development of the proof.

</details>

*Oliver Siebert*

TL;DR: 本文研究了ℝ^d中连续费米子在对势相互作用下的海森堡动力学，证明在一般非恒定对势、适当色散关系与外势假设下，该动力学在规范不变CAR代数上甚至不满足逐点范数连续性；进而由自动连续性论证得出：CAR代数及其规范不变子代数均不被该动力学所保持，且非不变性在几乎所有时刻发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解连续费米系统在相互作用下的代数动力学结构，特别是CAR代数在海森堡演化下的不变性问题，这对量子多体理论和代数量子场论中的时间演化定义具有基础意义。

Method: 运用算子代数方法，结合自动连续性定理（automatic continuity theorem）与规范不变CAR代数的结构性质，分析海森堡动力学在范数拓扑下的连续性；通过构造反例与谱条件分析，证明动力学映射在逐点范数意义下不连续。

Result: 证明了在广泛物理可实现的相互作用（非恒定对势）、色散关系及外势条件下，海森堡动力学在规范不变CAR代数上不具逐点范数连续性；从而推出CAR代数及其规范不变子代数均非动力学不变，且该非不变性发生在勒贝格几乎处处的时间点。

Conclusion: 连续费米系统的相互作用海森堡动力学在代数层面表现出深刻的拓扑病态性——即使在物理上自然的假设下，标准CAR代数框架亦无法容纳该动力学，揭示了连续模型与离散格点模型在代数动力学性质上的本质差异。

Abstract: We study the Heisenberg dynamics of continuum fermions in $\IR^d$ interacting through a pair potential. For a large class of nonconstant pair potentials and under suitable assumptions on the dispersion relation and external potential, we prove that the dynamics fails to be pointwise-norm continuous even on the gauge-invariant CAR algebra. An automatic-continuity argument then shows that neither the CAR algebra nor its gauge-invariant subalgebra is invariant under the dynamics, with non-invariance occurring for almost all times.

</details>


### [142] [Classical BV Pushforward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34533)
*Xin Geng,Andrey Losev,Vyacheslav Lysov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了经典BV pushforward，并证明其两个核心性质：(1) 将全空间上的经典主方程（CME）解映射为小空间上的CME解；(2) 在孤立临界点条件下，将BV规范等价的解映射为BV规范等价的解。文末给出示例与应用讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个在经典BV形式主义下保持CME结构的下降构造（pushforward），以支持量子化程序中的背景场方法、约化与有效作用量构造。

Method: 基于BV代数与余微分结构，利用经典BV拉格朗日子流形的几何框架，结合临界点分析与规范同伦论技术定义pushforward，并通过同调论证与局部坐标计算验证其性质。

Result: 严格定义了经典BV pushforward算子；证明其保持CME解性；证明其在孤立临界点处保持BV规范等价类；给出有限维及场论示例（如Chern–Simons约化）。

Conclusion: 经典BV pushforward是连接不同尺度BV结构的协变工具，为经典极限下的量子有效作用量、对称性约化与反常匹配提供代数基础。

Abstract: We define the classical BV pushforward and establish its two main properties. First, we prove that it maps solutions of the classical master equation (CME) on the total space to solutions of the CME on the smaller space. Second, we prove that, for isolated critical points, it maps BV canonically equivalent solutions to BV canonically equivalent solutions. We illustrate the construction with examples and discuss its applications.

</details>


### [143] [Scattering Measures and Information Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34721)
*Tatsuro Oda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将散射实验建模为参数化的有限测度，并基于Fisher度量构建其信息几何；通过分解总质量与归一化形状，分离出散射强度变化与结果重分布对Fisher信息的贡献；测量过程由（亚）概率核表示，其具有Fisher信息收缩性；该框架统一刻画实验操作，并衔接最优实验设计理论；同时指出该有限测度结构自然出现于探测不完全的量子测量中，厘清了从量子测量到经典统计的层次关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一个统一的信息几何框架来刻画散射实验，弥合量子测量、经典统计与最优实验设计之间的概念鸿沟，尤其处理探测效率不足等非理想测量情形。

Method: 采用参数化有限测度描述散射实验的结果空间；引入Fisher信息度量其参数依赖性；对测度进行总质量-形状分解；用（子）概率核建模测量过程；分析Fisher度量在核作用下的单调性（信息收缩）。

Result: 证明Fisher信息可分解为强度项与形状项；任意（亚）概率核导致Fisher信息非增；散射测度的信息几何与最优实验设计中的D-最优性等准则直接对应；不完全探测的POVM可自然嵌入该有限测度框架。

Conclusion: 散射实验的本质结构是参数化有限测度，其信息几何由Fisher度量刻画；该结构为经典与量子测量提供了统一的信息论语言，并为实验优化与探测建模提供严格数学基础。

Abstract: We formulate scattering experiments in terms of parameterized finite measures on outcome spaces and develop the associated information geometry defined by the Fisher metric. The decomposition of a scattering measure into total mass and normalized shape separates Fisher information into contributions from changes in overall scattering strength and redistribution among outcomes. Measurement processes are represented by probability or sub-probability kernels acting on the scattering measure. Such kernels contract Fisher information, placing a broad class of experimental operations within a common information-theoretic framework. This formulation connects scattering experiments to the theory of optimal experimental design through the Fisher geometry of scattering measures. The same finite-measure structure arises naturally in quantum measurements with incomplete detection, clarifying its place in the hierarchy from quantum measurement to classical outcome statistics.

</details>


### [144] [Notes on Magnetostatic Interactions in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34766)

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖本构关系（包括以太关系）的静磁学紧凑表述，基于磁偶极子分布与矢量势场相互作用的势能泛函，通过虚功原理导出力学力泛函，其中包含洛伦兹力密度、开尔文力密度、一个非对称主动应力张量（其反对称部分对应力学偶极密度）以及压力项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不依赖于介质本构关系（尤其是有争议的以太关系）的静磁力学基础框架，统一处理磁偶极子系统的能量与力学响应。

Method: 采用变分方法：首先定义由电流密度势场表征的磁偶极子分布与外矢量势场耦合的势能泛函；继而应用虚功原理（虚功等于势能时间导数的负值），对偶极子分布的虚拟运动进行变分，导出广义力学力泛函。

Result: 得到一个完整的磁体力泛函，其密度包含四项：(i) 洛伦兹力密度 J × B，(ii) 开尔文力密度 (M · ∇)B，(iii) 一个非对称主动应力张量 σ^a，其反对称部分给出力学偶极密度 m × B，(iv) 一个标量压力项 p。该结果在无本构假设下自洽成立。

Conclusion: 静磁相互作用的力学效应可完全由矢量势与电流密度势场的几何耦合决定，无需引入磁化强度 M 或磁导率 μ 的本构假设；非对称应力项揭示了磁介质中固有的微观转动自由度与宏观力-偶统一描述的必要性。

Abstract: A compact formulation of magnetostatics is presented. Without using constitutive relations, including the aether relations, an expression is proposed for the potential energy of a region carrying a distribution of magnetic dipoles under a vector potential field, where the dipole distribution is specified by a current density potential field. Assuming that during a virtual motion of the dipole distribution the virtual work expended by the field is equal to minus the time derivative of the potential energy, an expression for the mechanical force functional is derived. In addition to the Lorentz and Kelvin force densities, the force functional contains an asymmetric active stress field, the skew-symmetric part of which is the mechanical couple density, and a pressure term.

</details>


### [145] [A renormalized Lee Model: resolvent and spectral analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34980)
*Claudio Cacciapuoti,Federica Muscolino,Diego Noja,Andrea Posilicano*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建并分析了一个三维有质量Lee模型的重正化版本，通过抽象构造明确定义了其重正化哈密顿量的算子定义域、作用方式及Krein型预解式；利用总激发数守恒将全哈密顿量谱分析约化为各激发扇区上的算子 pencil（起Weyl函数作用）的谱研究；证明了扇区化HVZ型本质谱公式在前两个扇区严格成立，并在附加条件下推广至所有扇区；在自由/单激发阈值以下，高扇区点谱由一个非负自伴Birman–Schwinger算子刻画；对双激发及以上扇区，在重正化能隙≥玻色子质量时无亚阈值谱；若能隙更小，则在弱耦合条件下亦无亚阈值谱，并给出两激发扇区存在简单束缚态的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严格处理Lee模型中紫外发散问题，建立具有明确定义域与谱性质的三维重正化哈密顿量，并系统研究其激发结构依赖的谱分布，特别是本质谱与束缚态的存在性。

Method: 采用抽象算子理论方法构造重正化哈密顿量，利用总激发数守恒实现扇区分解，引入算子值Weyl函数（即激发依赖的算子 pencil）刻画谱，结合Krein型预解式、HVZ定理推广及Birman–Schwinger原理分析点谱与本质谱。

Result: 1) 构造出具有良好定义域与Krein型预解式的重正化三维Lee模型哈密顿量；2) 证明扇区化HVZ型本质谱公式在前两扇区严格成立，其余扇区在显式条件下成立；3) 在亚阈值区域，各高扇区点谱由非负自伴Birman–Schwinger算子完全刻画；4) 双激发及以上扇区在重正化能隙≥m_b或弱耦合下无亚阈值谱；5) 在能隙<m_b且弱耦合时，给出两激发扇区存在唯一简单束缚态的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维Lee模型提供了首个兼具严格算子构造与完整扇区化谱分析的重正化框架，揭示了激发结构对本质谱与束缚态的决定性影响，并建立了适用于强耦合量子场论模型的新型谱分析范式。

Abstract: We construct and analyze a renormalized three-dimensional massive Lee model; its renormalized Hamiltonian is defined intrinsically through an abstract construction which explicitly provides the operator domain, action and a Kre\uın-like resolvent formula. Conservation of the total excitation number reduces the spectral analysis of the full Hamiltonian to a sector-wise study of the spectral profile of an explicit operator pencil, playing the role of an operator-valued Weyl function. We construct essential spectrum components in a sector generated by lower excitation sectors and obtain the inclusion in the expected sector-wise HVZ type formula; the full formula is proved in the first two sectors and, under explicit conditions, in every sector. Below the free or one-excitation threshold, the point spectrum in every higher sector is described by a bounded self-adjoint nonnegative Birman--Schwinger operator. In every sector with at least two excitations we prove absence of sub-threshold spectrum for arbitrary coupling when the renormalized gap is not smaller than the boson mass, and under an explicit weak-coupling condition when it is smaller. In the latter case we also derive a sufficient condition for the existence of a simple bound state in the two-excitation sector.

</details>


### [146] [GR-FM: Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching for SDF-Based Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35006)
*Yuxin Ai,Zhichang Guo,Fanghui Song,Dazhi Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何正则化流匹配（GR-FM）的医学图像分割框架，利用符号距离场（SDF）建模目标结构，结合双调和正则项与Eikonal约束提升空间平滑性、结构一致性与距离场特性，在MoNuSeg、GlaS和DRIVE数据集上实现了高边界精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流匹配的生成式分割方法缺乏对恢复表示的空间正则性与距离场特性的显式约束，易导致空间振荡、边界偏移及细结构不连续；同时医学图像常具弱边界、不规则形状且标注稀缺。

Method: 将分割建模为从初始分布到目标隐式表示（SDF）分布的连续概率输运过程；采用流匹配与常微分方程实现确定性高效输运；引入双调和正则项与Eikonal约束以增强几何一致性；理论分析零水平集演化。

Result: 在MoNuSeg、GlaS、DRIVE数据集上，GR-FM在区域重叠（如Dice）与边界精度（如HD95、ASSD）指标上达到SOTA或竞争性能，降低结果方差，并仅需少量积分步数即保持稳定分割。

Conclusion: GR-FM通过将分割嵌入几何感知的连续输运框架，以SDF为隐式表示并施加微分几何约束，有效缓解了传统二值掩码建模带来的边界模糊与结构失真问题，为高精度医学图像分割提供了新范式。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains challenging in terms of accurate boundary localization and complex-structure preservation, as target regions often exhibit weak boundaries, fine-grained structures, and irregular shapes, while high-quality images and precise annotations are usually limited. Existing generative segmentation approaches based on Flow Matching mainly learn velocity fields in the state space but lack explicit constraints on the spatial regularity and distance-field properties of the recovered representation. This limitation may lead to spatial oscillations, boundary displacement, and discontinuities in fine structures. To address these issues, we propose an image-conditioned Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching framework, termed GR-FM, which formulates segmentation as a continuous probability transport process from an initial distribution to a target implicit representation distribution. Instead of directly modeling binary masks, GR-FM adopts the SDF to describe the target structure, where each pixel is represented by its signed distance to the object boundary, resulting in a continuous geometric field. Flow Matching and ordinary differential equations are then employed to achieve deterministic and efficient distribution transport. The training objective further incorporates a biharmonic regularization term and an Eikonal constraint to enhance the spatial smoothness, structural consistency, and distance-field characteristics of the recovered representation. Moreover, we analyze the continuous transport process under geometric constraints and investigate the evolution of the zero level set. Experiments conducted on the MoNuSeg, GlaS, and DRIVE datasets demonstrate that GR-FM achieves competitive performance in both region overlap and boundary accuracy, reduces performance variation, and maintains stable segmentation results with only a small number of integration steps.

</details>
